### [112] [Judging by the Cover: Cleaning LLM Truthfulness Benchmarks to Avoid Surface-Level Feature Leakage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13003)
*Foad Namjoo,Remy Ogasawara,Amirali Abdullah,Cullen Anderson,Narmeen Fatimah Oozeer,Jeff M. Phillips*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文指出二元真假基准可能因正确与错误答案存在系统性的表面特征差异而产生数据泄漏，使模型无需真正推理即可超过随机水平。作者提出Audit-Prune机制，通过移除最强化泄漏的答案对来清理数据集，并发布降低表面特征泄漏的TruthfulQA版本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二元选择型真实性基准通常要求模型在正确答案和错误答案之间进行选择，但答案之间若存在词汇、长度、格式等表面层面的系统差异，模型可能利用这些偏差完成预测，而非执行目标推理。因此，需要检测此类泄漏并建立可推广的数据集清理方法，以提升基准评测的有效性和可信度。

Method: 作者首先在TruthfulQA上构建仅使用六种表面特征的逻辑回归分类器，用于区分正确答案与错误答案，以检测表面特征泄漏；随后分析其他基准中是否存在类似伪特征。针对检测到的泄漏，作者提出Audit-Prune机制，识别并移除最能强化泄漏的答案对，从而在数据发布前降低可被下游分类器利用的表面偏差。

Result: 在TruthfulQA上，仅依赖六种表面特征的逻辑回归分类器就能以显著高于随机水平的准确率区分正确答案和错误答案，表明该基准存在可利用的表面特征泄漏。作者还发现其他基准中同样存在类似问题。经过清理后，发布的TruthfulQA版本中表面特征泄漏已降低至接近随机水平。

Conclusion: 二元真假基准中的表面特征差异会导致不依赖目标推理的虚假高性能，因此应在数据集发布前进行系统审计。Audit-Prune提供了一种通用的数据清理机制，可降低答案对之间的表面泄漏，使基准结果更能反映模型真正的推理与真实性判断能力。

Abstract: Binary-choice truth benchmarks ask models to choose between a correct and an incorrect answer, but if the two answers differ systematically in surface-level features, models can exceed chance without performing the intended reasoning. We show that this failure mode is detectable and can be exploited by downstream classifiers. In TruthfulQA, a simple six-feature logistic classifier achieves substantial accuracy in separating correct from incorrect answers. We further show that similar surface-level artifacts are present in additional benchmarks. To counteract this, we developed a general mechanism to clean them by removing the most leakage-reinforcing pairs. We release a version of TruthfulQA with surface-feature leakage reduced close to chance and provide a mechanism, Audit-Prune, so that the datasets can be cleaned before release.

</details>


### [113] [Tasks over Application Manuals: Revealing Gaps in Long-Horizon Procedural Reasoning for Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13005)
*Utkarsh Soni,Syed Shariyar Murtaza,Yifan Nie,Sachin Chandrasekhar,Eugene Wen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出TAM基准，用于评估语言模型在超长、规则密集且多步骤依赖的程序性推理任务上的可靠性。实验显示，即使采用检索增强、ReAct和智能体框架，模型精确匹配率仍很低，说明现有短链式推理基准可能高估了模型能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多跳推理基准通常只要求少量检索或推理步骤，难以反映现实任务中依据数百页手册、跨章节执行大量相互依赖规则的挑战。因此，作者希望建立一个更贴近真实程序执行场景的评测基准，以检验模型能否稳定、准确地遵循长程规则流程。

Method: 作者构建Tasks over Application Manuals（TAM）基准，收集两个实际应用领域的任务，并使用人工验证标签。每个任务都要求模型查阅包含数万条规则的权威手册，执行跨不同章节的多步、相互依赖程序，最终输出精确答案。评估了通用提示方法、检索增强生成、ReAct式提示，以及基于GPT-5的智能体框架基线。

Result: 最佳精确匹配率在两个任务上都很低：一个领域为1%，另一个领域为15.5%。这表明当前模型即使结合检索和智能体机制，也难以可靠完成长程、规则驱动的程序性推理。

Conclusion: TAM揭示了现有基准可能高估大型语言模型的推理能力，并指出“可靠遵循长篇规则化程序”是一个尚未充分解决的关键挑战。论文同时公开了完整数据和代码，便于后续研究复现与比较。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have achieved strong performance on a wide range of natural language tasks, and recent benchmarks suggest that they are increasingly adept at multi-hop reasoning. However, these benchmarks are typically short-horizon, requiring only a small number of retrieval or inference steps, and provide limited evidence of reliability on real-world tasks that involve following manuals spanning hundreds of pages with complex, interdependent guidelines. In this paper, we introduce Tasks over Application Manuals (TAM), a benchmark for evaluating long-horizon procedural reasoning. We construct TAM by curating real-world tasks from two domains: ICD-10-CM clinical coding (mapping medical conditions to diagnostic codes) and U.S. federal sentencing (computing crime sentencing guideline outcomes, specifically offense levels), with human-validated labels. Each task requires following an authoritative manual with tens of thousands of rules and executing a sequence of interdependent steps across different sections to produce an exact answer. We evaluate general-purpose prompting approaches, including retrieval-augmented generation, ReAct-style prompting, and an agent-harness baseline on GPT-5, and find that the best exact-match performance remains extremely low: 1% on ICD-10-CM coding and 15.5% on sentencing tasks. These results show that current benchmarks may overestimate LLM reasoning ability and miss a key challenge: reliably following long, rule-based procedures. The complete TAM data and code are publicly available.

</details>


### [114] [Kraken: LLM-based Speech-to-Speech Translation via Low-bitrate VQ and Dual-path Source Conditioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13045)
*Hayato Futami,Hassan Shahmohammadi,Tushar Dhyani,Alkis Koudounas,Raphaël Lafargue,Yosuke Kashiwagi,Quentin Jodelet,Emiru Tsunoo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出 Kraken，一种基于预训练大语言模型的端到端语音到语音翻译系统：使用低比特率语音 token 降低 LLM 预测难度，并通过以源语音为条件的 Autowave-X 解码器改善说话人身份与韵律迁移。在 15 万小时多语言、多任务语音数据上训练后，Kraken 的翻译质量优于 SeamlessM4T-Large v2 和 Qwen2.5-Omni，同时具备更好的说话人及韵律保持能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语音大语言模型用于语音到语音翻译时，主要面临两项问题：第一，LLM 难以高效预测高比特率语音 token；第二，模型通常依赖说话人身份和韵律严格对齐的 S2ST 训练数据，而这类数据获取成本高、规模有限。论文旨在降低语音 token 建模难度，并放宽对严格对齐训练数据的依赖，同时保留翻译内容之外的非语言信息。

Method: 1. 使用基于单层向量量化的低比特率语音 token，并以重建自监督学习（SSL）特征为训练目标，从而降低 LLM 的输出建模负担。2. 引入独立的 token-to-waveform 解码器 Autowave-X，并将源语音作为条件输入，以增强说话人身份和韵律等非语言信息的迁移能力。3. 构建 Kraken：在预训练 Qwen3-8B 上增加语音特征输入与低比特率 token 输出模块，最后由 Autowave-X 声码器生成波形。模型使用 15 万小时多语言、多任务语音数据训练。

Result: 实验表明，Kraken 的语音翻译质量优于 SeamlessM4T-Large v2 和 Qwen2.5-Omni；同时，在说话人身份迁移和韵律迁移方面也取得更好的表现。摘要未提供具体评价指标、数据集划分或统计显著性结果，因此无法进一步判断提升幅度及其稳健性。

Conclusion: 低比特率语音 token 与源语音条件化波形解码器能够有效互补：前者提升语音 token 的 LLM 建模可行性，后者缓解训练数据中说话人和韵律对齐不足的问题。Kraken 证明了预训练 LLM 可以作为高质量 S2ST 系统的核心，并在翻译性能和非语言信息保持之间取得较好平衡。

Abstract: Speech-to-speech translation (S2ST) has advanced significantly with speech LLMs, offering the potential for joint optimization and preserving non-linguistic information. However, these models struggle with predicting high-bitrate speech tokens in LLMs, and face the challenge of relying on S2ST training data with ideally aligned speaker identity and prosody. We propose using low-bitrate tokens based on single-layer vector quantization, trained to reconstruct self-supervised learning (SSL) features. We also employ a separate token-to-waveform decoder named Autowave-X, which is also conditioned on the source speech to improve non-linguistic transfer, thereby relaxing the training data constraints. With the integration of these techniques, we propose an S2ST model named Kraken, which augments a pre-trained LLM with speech feature inputs and the low-bitrate token outputs, followed by Autowave-X vocoder. We built the model upon Qwen3-8B and trained it using 150k hours of multilingual and multitask speech data. We demonstrated that our model exhibited better translation quality than SeamlessM4T-Large v2 and Qwen2.5-Omni, along with improved speaker and prosody transfer capabilities.

</details>


### [115] [Expert-Space Exploration in MoE Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13058)
*Hongyi He,Zhenghao Lin,Xiao Liu,Peng Cheng,Yan Lu,Yeyun Gong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出ESRL，一种面向混合专家（MoE）模型的专家空间探索强化学习方法。该方法通过受控扰动专家路由增加rollout多样性，同时保留高置信专家、限制候选专家范围、依据路由器熵自适应扰动强度，并在策略优化阶段重放rollout中的专家路径，以缓解路由不匹配。实验表明，ESRL在多种MoE架构和数学、科学、代码任务上均优于对比方法；在Qwen3-30B-A3B上，相比GRPO，Pass@1和Pass@8分别提升3.2和4.5个百分点，且无需额外采样或计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MoE强化学习工作主要关注优化稳定性和训练效率，将专家选择视为固定组件。然而，专家路由决定稀疏计算路径，并直接影响模型输出分布，因此可能成为提升rollout多样性的额外手段。直接随机扰动路由虽可改变输出、类似提高解码温度，但可能选择不合适的专家并显著降低rollout质量。本文旨在利用MoE路由结构实现安全、有效且低成本的探索。

Method: 提出Expert-Space Exploration Reinforcement Learning（ESRL）。核心机制包括：1）保留高置信专家作为锚点；2）仅在合理的候选专家池中进行随机路由，避免激活不适合的专家；3）根据路由器熵自适应调整扰动强度，降低过度扰动风险；4）记录rollout阶段实际采用的专家路径，并在策略优化阶段重放这些路径，以缓解扰动造成的路由不匹配。

Result: 在采用top-K、top-1和共享专家路由的多种MoE骨干模型，以及数学、科学和代码任务上，ESRL均取得最佳或领先性能，且不增加额外采样和计算成本。在Qwen3-30B-A3B上，ESRL相较GRPO的平均Pass@1提升3.2个百分点，Pass@8提升4.5个百分点。进一步的专家利用率和训练动态分析表明，显式利用MoE路由结构有助于强化学习训练。

Conclusion: 专家路由不仅是MoE模型的固定执行机制，也可以作为强化学习中的结构化探索空间。ESRL通过质量受控的专家扰动扩大rollout多样性，并通过路径重放保证训练与采样的一致性，从而在不增加计算开销的情况下提升MoE模型的强化学习效果。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has become central to post-training of large language models. Recent advances in RL for Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models have primarily focused on improving optimization stability and training efficiency, while treating the expert selection as a fixed component. Since routing determines the sparse computation paths that induce output distributions, expert selection offers an additional source of rollout diversity. Through empirical analysis, we find that perturbing expert routing effectively alters model output and increases rollout diversity, which is similar to increasing the decoding temperature. However, direct perturbation can activate unsuitable experts and substantially degrade rollout quality. Motivated by these observations, we introduce Expert-Space Exploration Reinforcement Learning (ESRL), an architecture-aware framework that explicitly explores the expert-routing space of MoE models. ESRL preserves high-confidence experts as anchors, and restricts stochastic routing to a plausible candidate pool, thereby retaining reliable computation paths. The perturbation strength is further adapted according to router entropy to avoid over-perturbation. To mitigate the routing mismatch introduced by perturbation, ESRL records the expert paths used during rollout and replays them during policy optimization. Experiments demonstrate that ESRL achieves the best performance across MoE backbones with top-K, top-1, and shared-expert routing, as well as across mathematics, science, and code tasks without additional sampling or computational cost. Specifically, ESRL on Qwen3-30B-A3B achieves the best among all compared methods, improving average Pass@1 and Pass@8 over GRPO by 3.2 and 4.5 percentage points, respectively. Further analyses of expert utilization and training dynamics provide insights into how exploiting MoE-specific routing structure benefits RL training.

</details>


### [116] [Continue, Adapt, or Yield: In-Turn Adaptation to Overlapping Speech in Full-Duplex Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13117)
*Yunqi Lu,Tyler Baumgartner,Nikhil Johri,Brandon Tai,Candice Fan,Luc Debaupte,Ruben Aguilar,Bill Wang,Yi Zhong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出 Duplex Cue，一种评估全双工语音智能体“在保持发言的同时吸收听者贡献并调整内容”的方法。该方法区分听者意图与说话者行为，并在真实人类对话和 PersonaPlex 模型之间进行比较。结果显示，人类在协作性提示下的适应率为 68.2%，模型仅为 34.8%，表明仅评估继续说话或停止说话不足以衡量自然语音交互能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有全双工评估通常将系统反应简化为二元选择：继续说话或停止说话。这种划分无法描述人类对话中的常见行为，即说话者在不让出话轮的情况下，吸收听者刚刚提供的信息，例如补充缺失词、修正错误或澄清内容。因此，论文旨在建立一种能够衡量“话轮内适应”（in-turn adaptation）的评估框架，以更准确地反映语音智能体对听者贡献的响应能力。

Method: 论文提出 Duplex Cue 评估方法。该方法将两个维度分离：一是听者意图，包括附和反馈（backchannel）、协作性贡献（collaboration）和打断（interruption）；二是当前说话者的行为，包括不变地继续说、在当前话轮内进行适应、以及让出话轮。其中，适应不仅包括对听者的确认，也包括对正在表达内容的修订。研究使用非脚本化英语对话中的 300 个经人工确认的提示，并在筛选出当前说话者于提示出现时仍在发言且两种条件下均有可评分响应后，保留 208 对样本。随后比较真实人类回应与 PersonaPlex 在重放听者音频条件下生成的续说。

Result: 在 66 个协作性样本中，真实说话者有 68.2% 的情况进行了话轮内适应，而 PersonaPlex 仅为 34.8%。模型在其余情况下主要表现为不作调整地继续说话（42.4%）或让出话轮（22.7%）。结果说明，模型虽然能够维持发言，但较少将听者的贡献整合到当前内容中，其交互行为与人类存在明显差距。

Conclusion: 研究表明，自然语音交互的评估不能只关注智能体是否继续说话或停止说话，还必须衡量其是否能够理解并吸收听者的即时贡献，在保持话轮的同时进行确认、修正或内容调整。Duplex Cue 为评估全双工语音智能体的细粒度适应能力提供了一个补充性框架；同时，当前 PersonaPlex 在协作性话轮内适应方面仍有较大提升空间。

Abstract: Full-duplex evaluation often emphasizes whether an agent keeps speaking or stops. That binary cannot express a third response humans use routinely: continuing to speak while incorporating what the listener just contributed. The contribution may be a missing word, a correction or a clarification. We introduce Duplex Cue, an evaluation of this \emph{in-turn adaptation} in full-duplex voice agents. Duplex Cue separates listener intent (backchannel, collaboration, or interruption) from speaker behavior: continuing unchanged, adapting within the turn, or yielding. Adaptation includes acknowledgment as well as content revision. In a single-model case study using 300 human-confirmed cues from unscripted English conversations, we compare recorded human responses with PersonaPlex continuations generated while replaying the listener's audio. We retain 208 pairs with the ongoing speaker active at cue onset and a scorable response in each condition. On the 66 collaborative pairs, recorded speakers adapt in 68.2\% of cases, compared with 34.8\% for PersonaPlex. The model otherwise continues unchanged (42.4\%) or yields (22.7\%). These findings show why evaluating natural voice interaction requires measuring how an agent responds to a listener's contribution as well as whether it keeps speaking.

</details>


### [117] [SAS: Simple Attention Sparsification via End-to-End Optimization of Context Ranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13141)
*Zhiwei Li,Lei Zhu,Hao Gu,Xiang Hu,Yan Wang,Haitao Mi,Sirui Han,Leo Liang,Zhijiang Guo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出 Simple Attention Sparsification（SAS），通过将选择器的连续分数以对数门控形式注入注意力 softmax，使语言建模损失能够端到端更新上下文选择器，从而在固定稀疏注意力预算下学习更符合预测效果的上下文排序；实验表明其在推理、长上下文理解和智能体任务上优于现有可训练稀疏注意力方法，尤其在紧预算下优势明显。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练 Transformer 的注意力计算具有二次复杂度，注意力稀疏化通过为每个查询选择少量上下文 token 或 block 来降低成本。现有可训练方法通常先用轻量选择器打分，再进行不可导的硬 Top-K 选择，因此语言建模损失无法直接优化选择器，只能蒸馏原始模型的稠密注意力分布。然而，稠密注意力权重排序并不等价于固定注意力预算下对预测结果最重要的上下文排序，导致有限预算可能被分配给相对无用的上下文单元。

Method: 提出 SAS，一种带门控的稀疏注意力机制。训练时将选择器的连续分数以对数形式注入注意力 logits，并置于 attention softmax 内，使语言建模损失可以通过标准反向传播直接更新选择器。方法中的关键设计包括：1）将门控放在 softmax 内部并采用 log 形式；2）使用归一化 softmax gate，以校准历史上下文与始终保留的当前 block 之间的权重；3）保留连续选择器分数，使模型学习上下文单元的相对优先级，而不只是学习硬选择结果。此外，作者实现了兼容 FlashAttention 风格计算的内存高效 Triton kernel，以支持长序列训练。

Result: 在推理、长上下文理解和智能体任务上，SAS 在不同注意力预算下均稳定优于现有可训练稀疏注意力基线；当预算较紧时，性能提升尤其显著。结果表明，SAS 能够学习更适合下游预测任务的上下文排序，并更有效地利用有限的注意力预算。

Conclusion: SAS 通过让语言建模损失直接监督上下文选择，解决了稀疏注意力中选择器目标与下游预测影响不一致的问题。其核心机制简单，但通过 softmax 内对数门控、门控归一化和连续分数保留等设计实现了有效训练，并在多类任务中展现出较强的效率与性能优势。

Abstract: Post-training attention sparsification reduces the quadratic cumulative attention cost of pretrained Transformers by selecting a small set of context units (tokens or blocks) for each query. Existing trainable methods usually use a lightweight selector to score context units, followed by hard Top-K selection that blocks gradients from the language modeling loss. Consequently, these methods commonly distill layer-wise dense attention distributions. Although this encourages the selector to rank context units by dense attention weights in the original model, the ranking is not directly aligned with their impact on predictions under a fixed attention budget (i.e., the number of attended context units per query), potentially wasting the limited budget on less useful units. To address this misalignment, we propose Simple Attention Sparsification (SAS), a gated sparse attention mechanism that optimizes context ranking end-to-end with the language modeling loss. The key idea is to inject the selector's continuous scores into attention logits during training, allowing the loss to update the selector through standard backpropagation. We identify several choices crucial for this simple design to work well in practice: placing the gate inside the attention softmax in log form, using normalized softmax gates to calibrate historical context against the always-retained current block, and preserving continuous selector scores so the model learns relative priorities rather than only hard selections. To support long-sequence training, we implement a memory-efficient Triton kernel that integrates SAS into FlashAttention-style computation. Across reasoning, long-context understanding, and agentic tasks, SAS consistently outperforms trainable sparse attention baselines across attention budgets, with especially large gains under tight budgets, demonstrating more effective context ranking for downstream tasks.

</details>


### [118] [Type Diversity Enables Transformers to Generalise Compositionally](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13144)
*Anssi Moisio,Mathias Creutz,Mikko Kurimo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究组合泛化中词汇泛化与结构泛化难度差异的来源，指出差异主要与数据集中的类型多样性有关，而非Transformer架构本身。作者通过改造COGS和SLOG数据集，控制词汇与结构类型多样性，发现两者的类型多样性都与组合泛化性能同等相关，并进一步考察其他数据集属性及逻辑语义表面形式的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究认为，对Transformer而言，结构泛化比词汇泛化更困难。本文质疑这一结论是否源于模型的固有能力差异，还是由于既有数据集中词汇类型多样性较高、结构类型多样性较低所造成的偏差。同时，作者希望检验“复合分歧（compound divergence）”是否能够解释组合泛化任务的难度，并分析其他数据集设计因素的影响。

Method: 作者将类型多样性定义为某类构造器的数量，而不是具体词语组合的数量。基于已发表的COGS和SLOG数据集，使用Grammatical Framework构建语言学上更加多样化的变体，并分别调节词汇类型和结构类型的多样性。随后比较不同类型多样性条件下的组合泛化表现，并进一步研究非目标新结构类型的多样性，以及逻辑语义表示格式的表面属性等因素。

Result: 实验结果表明，类型多样性与词汇泛化和结构泛化的表现均存在相关性，且作用程度相当。这支持了作者的核心假设：结构泛化更困难并非Transformer固有的限制，而可能是既有数据集类型多样性不平衡导致的。研究还发现，该结果与先前关于复合分歧能够解释组合泛化难度的观点存在矛盾；此外，其他类型的多样性和逻辑语义格式的表面特征也会影响模型表现。

Conclusion: 组合泛化难度不能简单归因于词汇与结构类别本身，也不能仅凭既有数据集上的结果断言Transformer缺乏结构泛化能力。数据集中的类型多样性是一个关键混杂因素，应在评估组合泛化时进行平衡和控制。未来研究还需系统考虑数据集的整体类型分布及语义表示的表面形式，以获得更可靠的泛化结论。

Abstract: Compositional generalisation has been divided into lexical and structural generalisation. Previous work has found that structural generalisation is harder than lexical for Transformers. We propose that this difference is not inherent to Transformers, but due to the high diversity of lexical types and low diversity of structural types in the specific datasets of these previous works. By type diversity we mean the number of different constructors of that type, instead of, for example, the specific word combinations that might populate the structure. To test this, we vary the amounts of type diversity of lexical and structural types in previously published datasets. We create linguistically diverse variants of the COGS and SLOG datasets using Grammatical Framework. We find that type diversity correlates with compositional generalisation equally in lexical and structural test cases, supporting our hypothesis. We note a contradiction with the proposition in previous work that compound divergence explains the difficulty in compositional generalisation tasks. We further investigate the effects of other dataset properties on compositional generalisation, such as the diversity of types other than the novel test structure, and surface properties of the logical semantics format.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [119] [Occamy-1.0: Open Pareto-frontier 35B Intelligence for Co-work](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11977)
*Wenhui Chen,Shiwen Cheng,Hao Dong,Chenda Duan,Ruixiang Feng,Zhong Guan,Boqiang Guo,Xueyuan Han,Haojie Hao,Liangmeng Huang,Zhelong Huang,Xinke Kong,Hongyu Li,Jiazheng Li,Junbo Li,Qingchuan Li,Yukun Lian,Chang Liu,Tianyu Liu,Zicheng Liu,Shuyi Ouyang,Yijun Pan,Kunyu Shi,Xiaojun Tang,Bingquan Wang,Kesu Wang,Yuchen Wang,Sibo Wei,Sicong Xie,Xiaoying Xing,Yi Xu,Zhijun Xu,Hongwei Xue,Qingcheng Zeng,Di Zhang,Guannan Zhang,Haochen Zhang,Tianlong Zhang,Tianyu Zhao,Tianyu Zhao,Yanjun Zheng,Jialong Zhu,Zijian Zou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 Occamy-1.0：一个面向协同工作（co-work）智能体的高性价比模型，通过执行导向数据、可复现长程轨迹和分阶段后训练，在多项工作流基准上取得接近更大前沿模型的性能，同时显著降低成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协同工作智能体需要连续完成信息搜集、工具调用、编程和文件操作等复杂流程。其实际价值不仅取决于单次推理能力，还取决于整个任务流程中的累计成本与延迟。许多日常工作步骤更依赖状态跟踪、任务协调、错误恢复和持续执行，而非最高级别的复杂推理，因此需要专门优化的高性价比模型。

Method: 作者基于经过后训练的 Qwen3.6-35B-A3B checkpoint，构建执行导向的数据与环境，收集来自多个 harness 的可回放长时程任务轨迹，并采用分阶段后训练，以培养和整合互补的执行能力。评测覆盖多项 co-work 基准，并辅以工具调用、编程和指令遵循测试；同时依据预先声明的评测与定价协议，分析性能—成本 Pareto 前沿。

Result: Occamy-1.0 在广泛的 co-work 基准上持续位居同等规模模型前列，并在若干任务上接近或保持了与显著更大前沿模型的竞争力。按四个代表性基准的综合结果及指定价格计算，该模型位于观测到的性能—成本 Pareto 前沿的低成本拐点。工具调用、编程和指令遵循评测表明，其专门化并未明显牺牲通用智能体能力。

Conclusion: 论文表明，围绕真实执行流程进行数据构建、环境训练和分阶段后训练，可以在较小模型规模下获得较强的协同工作智能体能力，并实现更优的成本—性能平衡。发布模型权重及部分训练数据，有助于推动实用型 co-work agent 与智能体后训练研究。不过，摘要未提供具体基准分数、成本数值、消融实验或失败案例，因此对方法贡献和优势幅度的判断仍需结合正文。

Abstract: Co-work agents execute complex workflows that combine information gathering, tool use, coding, and file manipulation across many model invocations. Because cost and latency accumulate over the full episode, their practical value depends not only on peak capability but also on how efficiently that capability is delivered. Yet many steps in everyday work emphasize state tracking, coordination, recovery, and follow-through rather than frontier-scale reasoning. We present Occamy-1.0, a cost-efficient co-work model obtained by further training the post-trained Qwen3.6-35B-A3B checkpoint. We construct execution-grounded data and environments, capture replayable long-horizon trajectories across multiple harnesses, and use staged post-training to develop and consolidate complementary execution capabilities. Across a broad suite of co-work benchmarks, Occamy-1.0 is consistently among the strongest comparably sized models and remains competitive with substantially larger frontier systems on several tasks. Under our stated evaluation and pricing protocol, its aggregate performance across four representative benchmarks places it at the low-cost knee of the observed cost--performance Pareto frontier. Supporting evaluations in tool calling, coding, and instruction following further show that this specialization preserves broad agentic capability. We release the model weights and a subset of the training data to support research on practical co-work agents and agentic post-training.

</details>


### [120] [Harness or Model? Isolating the Harness Effect in Agentic Coding with a Contamination-Controlled Private Suite](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11987)
*Mohsen Arjmandi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文比较同一语言模型搭配不同智能体编程框架的效果与成本。结果显示，厂商原生框架并未在平均任务成功率上稳定优于通用框架；性能受任务类型影响，成本结论还受缺失用量记录影响，需谨慎解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究检验一个常见假设：语言模型与其供应商原生 harness 的组合是否比通用 harness 更能完成软件工程任务。作者还关注正确性、完成时间和每个成功任务的成本，并纠正先前版本中的遥测计费语义错误。

Method: 在私有、污染受控的256项仓库任务与截止日期后的竞赛任务上开展配对对照实验。主要比较为：Claude Opus 4.8 分别搭配 claude-agent-sdk 与 deepagents，GPT-5.5 分别搭配 openai-codex SDK 与 deepagents；另设 Gemini 3.5 Flash 和 DeepSeek-V3.2 侧单元。计划800次运行，792次由隔离式评分器评估，并使用任务自助法置信区间、分层分析和标签置换检验。成本按冻结标价和实际单轮用量重算。

Result: 平均成功率没有显示任一 harness 的明确优势：Opus 4.8 的差异为−1.25个百分点，GPT-5.5 为+1.25个百分点，置信区间均跨越零。Opus 的仓库任务中原生 harness 落后9.0个百分点，而竞赛任务中领先23.7个百分点，但该分层是在观察数据后选择的，标签置换检验 p=0.003，不能直接视为确认性证据。81次达到时间上限的运行中有22次已产生通过评分的补丁，说明完成率与正确性并不等价。按观测用量估算，通用 harness 每个成功任务成本约为原生方案的1.3–1.6倍（Opus）和1.2倍（GPT）；但Anthropic账户有58次运行缺少用量记录，Opus成本比可能在0.7–2.3之间，账单排序无法确定。

Conclusion: 论文不支持“厂商原生 harness 普遍更优”的结论；优势可能依赖模型和任务类型。成本方面只能得出初步的观测用量结论，因缺失遥测而不稳健。论文的可信度增强点是开放编排器、评分器、重分析代码和派生汇总数据；主要局限是任务私有、部分分层事后确定、样本量有限，以及成本数据不完整。

Abstract: An agentic coding system couples a language model to a harness: the tools, prompts and control flow that turn a chat model into an autonomous software engineer. Vendors ship harnesses tuned to their own models, and practitioners assume the vendor-native pairing solves more tasks. We measure that assumption with paired same-model contrasts on a private, contamination-controlled suite of 256 repository and post-cutoff contest tasks. The same 80 tasks ran under claude-agent-sdk and under deepagents on claude-opus-4-8, and under the openai-codex SDK and deepagents on gpt-5.5, with gemini-3.5-flash and deepseek-v3.2 as side cells. 792 of 800 planned runs were graded by an isolated oracle. Neither contrast resolves an average advantage for either harness: -1.25 pp for Opus 4.8 (48.8% vs 50.0%, task-bootstrap 95% CI [-10.0, +7.5]) and +1.25 pp for GPT-5.5 (55.6% vs 54.4%, CI [-4.4, +6.9]). The Opus average combines opposite strata: the native harness trails by 9.0 pp on the 61 repository tasks and leads by 23.7 pp on the 19 contest tasks (label-permutation p = 0.003). The partition was chosen after seeing the data and needs a designed replication. Correctness and completion also separate: 22 of 81 runs cancelled at the wall-clock ceiling had produced a passing patch. Re-priced from raw per-turn usage at frozen list prices, the neutral harness cost 1.3 to 1.6 times as much per solved task on Opus 4.8 and 1.2 times on GPT-5.5. These are observed-usage estimates. On the Anthropic account 58 runs left no usage record, and allocating that spend to either cell would move the Opus ratio between 0.7 and 2.3, so the billed ordering is unresolved. This revision corrects an August 2026 manuscript whose cost figures rested on a usage-semantics defect in our own telemetry (Section 5.1). We release the orchestrator, grading oracle, reanalysis code and derived aggregates. The tasks stay private.

</details>


### [121] [Reading the Whole Heart: Latent-Attention Masked Autoencoders for Multimodal Cardiac Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12035)
*Andrea Agostini,Simon Böhi,Moritz Vandenhirtz,Samuel Ruiperez-Campillo,Max Krähenmann,Silke Mühlstedt,Irene Cannistraci,Ece Özkan Elsen,Julia E. Vogt,Thomas M. Sutter*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出LAMAE，一种面向心血管多模态数据的结构感知掩码自编码器，在患者级潜空间中联合建模ECG、超声心动图、胸片和临床变量，通过研究-视图-实体层级及共享潜在注意力实现跨模态交互，并在多项住院任务上优于多种基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医学基础模型通常按单一模态训练，或仅在微调、后训练阶段融合不同模态，因而未能充分利用临床诊断中自然存在的跨模态互补证据，也忽略了每种模态内部的层次结构。此外，真实住院数据存在观测数量不固定、模态缺失等问题，需要一种能够统一处理多模态结构和缺失输入的自监督预训练方法。

Method: 提出Latent-Attention Masked Autoencoders（LAMAE）。该方法采用多模态、结构感知的掩码自编码器，在自监督预训练阶段直接学习患者级表示，而不是在后处理中融合模态。其核心是共享潜在注意力模块，在研究（study）-视图（view）-实体（entity）层级的结构上进行潜空间信息交换，从而聚合数量可变的观测，并能够较稳健地处理缺失模态。模型在超过120万次MIMIC-IV住院记录上进行预训练。

Result: 在院内死亡率预测、ICD-10编码、DRG编码和住院时长预测等多模态住院任务中，LAMAE优于单模态预训练方法，以及具有竞争力的对比学习和视觉-语言模型基线。在单模态任务上，LAMAE仍保持竞争力；即使测试时仅提供单一模态，其性能优势仍然存在，表明联合建模模态内结构与模态间结构能够获得更稳健、可迁移的表示。

Conclusion: LAMAE证明了在医学多模态基础模型中，直接于潜空间建模跨模态关系，并同时利用各模态内部层次结构，是提升下游任务泛化能力和缺失模态鲁棒性的有效策略。该方法尤其适合真实临床环境中观测不完整、模态可用性不一致的场景。

Abstract: Cardiovascular diagnosis rests on integrating complementary modalities, like ECG, echocardiography, chest radiographs, and clinical variables, each capturing distinct but correlated aspects of cardiac physiology. Yet most medical foundation models remain modality-specific, combining modalities only for finetuning or post-training. This discards the cross-modal evidence clinicians naturally integrate and ignores the structure within each modality. We introduce Latent-Attention Masked Autoencoders (LAMAE), a multimodal, structure-aware masked autoencoder that jointly learns patient-level representations during self-supervised pretraining. Rather than fusing modalities post hoc, LAMAE exchanges information directly in the latent space through a shared latent-attention module operating over a study-view-entity hierarchy, enabling aggregation of variable observations and graceful handling of missing modalities. Pretrained on over 1.2 million MIMIC-IV hospital stays, LAMAE outperforms modality-specific pretraining and strong contrastive and vision-language baselines across multimodal hospital-stay tasks, such as in-hospital mortality, ICD-10 and DRG coding, and length of stay, while remaining competitive on unimodal tasks. These gains persist even when only a single modality is available at test time, showing that modeling both intra- and inter-modal structure yields more robust, transferable representations.

</details>


### [122] [Competence-Gated Pooling of Language Models and Priors for Event Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12101)
*Aditi Tiwari,Aashrith Bandaru,Heng Ji*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文研究如何判断语言模型预测相对于已有外部预测是否具有边际价值。作者提出能力门控机制，利用已解析结果估计不同领域的模型与外部信号权重，并通过收缩和再校准进行选择性组合。实验表明，该方法总体改善Brier得分，但在市场预测子集上主要选择依从市场，未带来显著增益；结果支持基于实证能力而非模型口头置信度进行模型使用和弃权决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合预测系统通常已经拥有市场、群体或统计预测，核心问题不是语言模型自身是否准确，而是它能否在已有外部预测之外提供额外信息。传统全局组合权重难以反映不同领域中的相对能力，也无法有效处理样本不足和不确定性。因此，论文旨在衡量语言模型相对于外部预测的边际价值，并据此决定何时使用、组合或忽略模型信号。

Method: 在Brier损失框架下，作者刻画模型与外部预测发生分歧时可能带来改进的条件，并推导使用领域特定池化权重相对于全局权重的收益。随后提出能力门控机制：基于已解析结果估计各领域的来源权重，将不确定的领域估计向全局权重收缩，再对池化预测进行重新校准。实验覆盖2,357个已解析二元问题和5个语言模型，并设置数据泄漏控制、泄漏安全的时间序列先验、FRED数据以及ForecastBench市场子集等比较。另以4个Qwen模型检验语言模型的口头置信度是否能识别其相对外部预测的优势。

Result: 能力门控将主要外部基线的Brier得分从0.0771降至0.0732，并显著优于全局预测组合。该收益在泄漏控制和结构化池化数据上的泄漏安全时间序列先验比较中仍然显著，FRED数据也提供了独立支持。然而，在官方ForecastBench市场子集上，门控机制主要依从市场预测，未取得显著改进。四个Qwen模型的口头置信度不能稳定识别模型何时优于外部预测，而基于结果估计的能力能够支持更好的弃权决策。

Conclusion: 论文表明，混合预测中的关键不是追求语言模型的绝对准确率，而是测量其相对于现有外部预测的边际能力。基于领域结果估计、收缩和再校准的能力门控，可以实现更有选择性的模型调用，并在多个数据设置下改善预测表现。但其效果依赖数据领域：当外部市场预测已经很强时，最优策略可能是大幅降低语言模型权重或直接弃用。

Abstract: In hybrid forecasting, a language model is often one of several available signals. A system may already have a market, crowd, or statistical forecast and must decide whether the model adds useful information or should be ignored. The relevant target is therefore not standalone model accuracy, but relative competence, defined as the model's marginal value beyond the available external forecast. Under Brier loss, we characterize when model disagreement can improve an external forecast and derive the gain from using domain-specific rather than global pooling weights. We then introduce a competence gate that estimates domain-level source weights from resolved outcomes, shrinks uncertain estimates toward a global weight, and recalibrates the pooled forecast. Across 2,357 resolved binary questions and five language models, the gate improves the main external baseline from 0.0771 to 0.0732 Brier and significantly outperforms global forecast combinations. The gain remains significant under leakage controls and against a leakage-safe time-series prior on the pooled structured set, with separate evidence on FRED. In contrast, the gate gives no significant improvement on the official ForecastBench market subset, where it largely defers to the market. Across four Qwen models, verbal confidence does not reliably identify when the model outperforms the external forecast, while outcome-estimated competence supports better abstention decisions. These results provide a practical approach for selective model use based on measured marginal value.

</details>


### [123] [Language Is an Insufficient Substrate for Quantitative Reasoning, and Consequential Domains Need Large Quantitative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12105)
*Reuben Vandeventer,David Imrem,David J. Wild*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出“大型量化模型”（Large Quantitative Model, LQM）这一模型范式，认为仅依赖语言模型无法可靠支持定价风险、资本配置、患者分诊和网络入侵遏制等关键量化决策，因为自然语言是对原始量化记录的有损且不可逆编码。LQM应直接面向量化记录，并满足信息完整性、可复现性、输出可追溯性和不确定性校准等要求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前应用机器学习普遍假设，大型语言模型能力的提升将自然带来关键量化决策能力的提升。本文质疑这一假设：语言模型训练所依赖的世界表征来自人类描述，而描述会丢失原始量化记录中的信息；一旦信息未被编码，后续模型无论规模多大都无法恢复。因此，在高后果决策场景中，需要重新审视模型输入表征及其能够提供的可靠性属性。

Method: 本文主要采用形式化论证与概念框架分析。作者将信息损失归因于模型训练所使用的表征，而非模型容量本身，并分析语言输入在关键量化任务中的结构性局限。进一步提出并归纳三项必要属性：结果可复现、每个输出均能追溯至生成它的源记录，以及经过校准的不确定性。在此基础上，作者定义一种区别于语言模型的模型类别——大型量化模型（LQM）。

Result: 文章得出两类核心结论：第一，基于语言描述的模型无法恢复描述阶段已经丢失的量化信息，扩大模型规模不能解决该问题；第二，语言作为基础载体在结构上无法充分保证可复现性、完整的输出谱系追踪和校准不确定性。因此，关键量化决策需要不同于LLM的模型范式，即LQM。

Conclusion: 本文主张，面向高风险量化决策的模型不能仅以语言建模能力或规模增长为核心，而应直接建立在量化记录之上，并将信息保真、可复现、可追溯和不确定性校准作为设计要求。LQM由此被提出为适合此类任务的独立模型类别。

Abstract: The prevailing assumption in applied machine learning is that progress on consequential quantitative decisions such as pricing risk, allocating capital, triaging patients, or containing a network intrusion will follow from progress in large language models (LLMs). A language model is trained on a representation of the world that was produced by human description; description is a lossy encoding of the quantitative record, and the loss is irreversible: no downstream model, at any scale, can recover from a description what the description did not encode. We formalize this as a property of the representation on which a model is trained rather than of the model capacity, and we identify three further properties that consequential settings demand of a model and that a language substrate cannot supply by construction: reproducibility, lineage from every output back to the source records that produced. it, and calibrated uncertainty. We argue that these properties define a distinct model class, which we call the Large Quantitative Model (LQM).

</details>


### [124] [DU-NO: A Parameter-Efficient Double U-Shaped Neural Operator for Phase-Resolving Wave Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12115)
*Enrique Hernandez Noguera,Md Meftahul Ferdaus,Nathan Cooper,Elias Ioup,Mahdi Abdelguerfi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出DU-NO，一种面向近岸波浪动力学预测的双U形神经算子。它仅在浅层编码器和解码器引入轻量卷积分支，在保持高频局部信息建模能力的同时显著降低参数量；在FUNWAVE-TVD基准上，DU-NO以3.64M参数取得最佳自回归滚动误差，相比U-FNO误差降低14.9%，参数减少10.8倍，并在浅水方程等任务上表现出良好泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相位分辨波浪模型FUNWAVE-TVD能够精确模拟波浪的浅化、折射和破碎，但计算成本过高，难以支持大规模集合预报、不确定性量化和实时预警。现有神经算子虽然具有加速潜力，但高精度模型通常参数量庞大，尤其难以有效保留波场中的高波数细节。因此，本文旨在设计一种兼顾精度、效率和高频信息建模能力的轻量神经算子。

Method: 提出Double U-shaped Neural Operator（DU-NO）。其核心是多尺度U形谱算子：仅在编码器和解码器最浅的两个层级附加轻量卷积U-Net分支，使全频局部建模路径集中处理仅存在于细网格上的高波数信息，而较粗、频带受限的层级保持纯谱操作。模型采用随网络深度递减的谱模态调度，将参数量控制在3.64M。实验比较六种采用相同训练设置的架构，并进一步进行频带分析和参数匹配对照，以区分架构优势与单纯参数规模优势。

Result: 在公开FUNWAVE-TVD基准上，DU-NO取得六种架构中最优的自回归滚动误差，相比U-FNO误差降低14.9%，参数量减少10.8倍。频带分析表明，其优势覆盖所有频段，包括传统截断谱算子容易失效的高波数频段。参数匹配实验显示，在约3.6M参数预算下，最佳基线仍落后DU-NO 28.6%，说明性能提升主要源于架构设计而非参数规模。迁移到二维Navier–Stokes任务时，DU-NO与最强基线相当；在PDEBench浅水方程滚动预测中则具有明显优势。

Conclusion: DU-NO通过将局部卷积建模能力有针对性地放置在细尺度、高波数信息最丰富的网络层级，实现了精度与效率的有效平衡。结果表明，轻量化并不必然牺牲复杂波场中的高频预测能力，DU-NO适合计算资源受限的长时滚动预测、集合模拟和实时预警场景，并具有一定的跨PDE泛化潜力。

Abstract: Phase-resolving wave models such as FUNWAVE-TVD are the accuracy standard for nearshore dynamics, resolving the shoaling, refraction, and breaking of individual waves, but their cost rules them out for the ensembles, uncertainty quantification, and real-time warning that operational forecasting demands. Neural operators promise solver-level accuracy at a fraction of that cost, yet on wave-dominated fields the accurate ones are large: hybrid spectral-convolutional operators such as U-FNO (the strongest baseline in our study after DU-NO) buy their fidelity with tens of millions of parameters. We introduce DU-NO (Double U-shaped Neural Operator), a multiscale U-shaped spectral operator that attaches lightweight convolutional U-Net branches only at its two shallowest encoder and decoder levels. The placement follows a sampling argument: high-wavenumber content exists only on fine grids, so the local, full-band pathways go where that content lives, while the coarse, band-limited levels stay purely spectral. A depth-decaying mode schedule holds the model to 3.64M parameters, an order of magnitude below U-FNO. On our publicly released FUNWAVE-TVD benchmark, DU-NO attains the best autoregressive rollout error of six identically trained architectures, improving on U-FNO by 14.9% with 10.8x fewer parameters, and a frequency-band analysis shows the gain holds across all bands, including the high-wavenumber band where truncated-spectral operators collapse. Parameter-matched controls confirm the gain is architectural: rescaled to the same 3.6M budget, the best baseline still trails DU-NO by 28.6%. The advantage carries beyond nearshore waves: DU-NO matches the strongest baselines on 2D Navier-Stokes and wins clearly on PDEBench shallow-water rollouts. Code, trained models, and evaluation artifacts are available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/duno-code-5A7B/.

</details>


### [125] [When Successful Knowledge Graph Edits Displace Correct Answers: Rank-Level Locality beyond Parameter Support](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12116)
*Yi-Cheng Lai,Jerry Wang,Hsin-Ling Hsu,Li-Chu Chi,Ya-Wen Teng,Hen-Hsen Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究知识图谱嵌入模型编辑中的排序位移问题，提出三层次审计框架，并比较多种编辑与保护策略。结果表明，单纯提升目标答案常会损害其他正确答案；支持正则化和截断排序保护可在修正成功率与局部性之间取得更好平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有知识图谱嵌入模型编辑的局部性测试，往往只检查复用被编辑参数的事实，可能遗漏正确答案在返回列表中的排序位移。即使目标答案被成功提升，也可能挤出其他正确答案，因此需要系统衡量位移的范围与严重程度。

Method: 提出一个通用的排序位移审计框架，分别考察：由被编辑参数支持的事实、目标查询的其他正确答案，以及共享同一关系的其他查询中的正确答案。进一步推导在提升目标分数的同时精确保持选定分数所需的维度与几何条件。在FB15k-237数据集上，使用DistMult和ComplEx，比较直接提升、严格保持、支持正则化实体编辑、排序截断保持等策略，并扩展考察不同维度、评分器、排序约定和学习型编辑器。

Result: 直接提升策略始终能将目标答案提升至前十，但仅有23.0%--23.2%的编辑没有造成损害。严格保持策略未观测到损害，但成功率仅为1.3%--1.4%。支持正则化实体编辑取得最高的联合成功率，为36.3%--37.7%；排序截断保持达到32.8%--34.7%，并将平均被位移答案数从约14个降至1.2个。实验还表明，局部性同时取决于受保护范围和编辑机制。

Conclusion: 知识图谱嵌入编辑不应只报告目标答案的修正成功率，还应同时报告排序位移的发生率及严重程度。局部性并非单一属性，而是由保护范围、模型评分器、排序规则和编辑方法共同决定；支持正则化与排序截断保护是更具实用性的折中方案。

Abstract: Editing a knowledge graph embedding (KGE) model to promote a desired answer can displace correct answers from the returned list. Locality tests based only on facts that reuse the edited parameter can miss this ranking effect. We introduce a common rank-displacement audit at three scopes: facts supported by the edited parameter, other correct answers to the target query, and correct answers across queries with the same relation. We also derive dimensional and geometric conditions for an update to improve the target while exactly preserving selected scores. On FB15k-237 with DistMult and ComplEx, direct promotion always moves the target into the top ten, but does so without damage in only 23.0--23.2\% of edits. Strict preservation causes no measured damage, yet succeeds in only 1.3--1.4\%. Support-regularized entity editing gives the highest joint success, 36.3--37.7\%, while rank-truncated preservation reaches 32.8--34.7\% and reduces the mean number of displaced answers from about 14 to 1.2. Experiments across dimensions, scorers, ranking conventions, and a learned editor show that locality depends on both the protected scope and the editing mechanism. KGE editing should therefore report correction success together with the incidence and severity of rank displacement.

</details>


### [126] [Mined from Scientific Literature: Process Schemas for Atomic Layer Deposition and Etching in Materials Science](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12139)
*Sameer Sadruddin,Eleni Poupaki,Alex Watkins,Bora Karasulu,Adriaan J. M. Mackus,Erwin Kessels,Sören Auer,Jennifer D'Souza*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出四个经领域专家评审的JSON Schema，用于统一描述原子层沉积（ALD）和原子层刻蚀（ALE）的实验与模拟过程，并通过语义量化单元本体（QUDT）和ORKG模板支持结构化文献抽取与发布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ALD和ALE相关研究在实验与模拟文献中的报告方式缺乏统一性，导致不同研究之间难以比较，也不利于机器可读、可复用和自动化处理。因此，需要建立覆盖材料、工艺条件、配置以及测量或预测结果的规范化数据模式。

Method: 使用schema-miner挖掘和整理领域信息，并借助schema-miner pro将模式与QUDT进行语义对齐和量化单位规范化；构建四个分别面向ALD实验、ALD模拟、ALE实验和ALE模拟过程的JSON Schema。随后比较各模式的范围、结构和语义基础，并通过模式引导的文献抽取及ORKG模板发布结构化记录，验证其应用价值。

Result: 形成了四个经过四位领域专家评审的JSON Schema，能够结构化表示ALD和ALE实验及模拟过程中的材料、工艺条件、系统配置和测量或预测结果。研究还展示了这些模式在文献结构化抽取和ORKG记录发布中的实际用法，并对其覆盖范围、组织结构和语义规范化程度进行了比较。摘要未提供定量性能指标或具体抽取准确率。

Conclusion: 该研究为ALD和ALE文献提供了统一、可机器处理且具有语义 grounding 的数据表示框架，有助于跨文献比较、知识复用和后续自动化分析。结合QUDT与ORKG，所提出的模式可作为材料科学领域结构化知识发布和文献挖掘的基础。

Abstract: Atomic layer deposition (ALD) and atomic layer etching (ALE) are reported heterogeneously across experimental and simulation literature in materials science, hindering comparison and machine-actionable reuse. We present four domain-expert-reviewed JSON Schemas for ALD and ALE experimental and simulation processes. Curated with schema-miner and grounded in QUDT using schema-miner pro, the schemas structure materials, process conditions, configurations , and measured or predicted results. We compare their scope, structure, and semantic grounding, and demonstrate their use for schema-guided literature extraction and publication of structured records through ORKG templates.

</details>


### [127] [Can LLMs in Draft-Verify-Revise Pipelines Resolve Deictic Ambiguity?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12162)
*Obinna I. Ekekezie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究多阶段 draft-verify-revise LLM 推理流程中的指示语义偏移（deictic shift）：不同阶段模型可能对“previous”等上下文依赖表达式产生不同指称，从而影响最终判断。实验显示模型性能差异显著，推理强度可改善部分模型表现，但低成本模型也可能更优；错误主要源于依赖表面线索而非执行操作性推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在 draft-verify-revise 流程中，上下文会跨阶段传递，不同 LLM 可能以不同方式解析“previous”等依赖上下文的表达式。若指称发生变化，验证和修订阶段可能建立在错误理解之上。现有工作缺乏对这一现象的系统测量，因此本文旨在评估模型识别正确指称、处理阶段间语义偏移以及进行元评估的能力，并为上下文工程提供实践建议。

Method: 构造包含10个基础例子的合成数据集，每个例子生成三种条件：分别操控 draft 阶段助手或 verify 阶段评分器是否正确解析表达式，并操控 revise 阶段元评估器所需的独立推理量。测试来自三家提供商的六个模型，覆盖21种推理强度配置；使用 e-values 进行序贯检验，同时开展去除评分器错误分类标签的消融实验。以平衡准确率（敏感度与特异度的非加权平均）评估性能，并由另一个 LLM 分析元评估器错误判决时给出的理由。

Result: 平衡准确率从0.156（低于随机水平）到接近完美不等。GPT-5.2在无推理配置下为0.156，在最高推理强度下提升至0.942；Gemini 3 Pro在所有推理强度下均高于0.94。低推理强度的 Gemini 3 Pro 性能超过高推理强度（xhigh）的 GPT-5.2，而单次试验成本约为其5%。元评估器出错时，通常依赖表面线索，而不是基于操作过程进行推理。

Conclusion: 多阶段 LLM 编排中的指示语义偏移是一个实际风险，且模型能力和推理配置对其影响显著。提高推理强度能够改善部分模型，但并非所有模型都需要高计算成本。设计 draft-verify-revise 流程时，应在每个阶段明确写出表达式的预期指称，避免让“previous”等上下文依赖表达式在阶段间产生歧义。

Abstract: Draft-verify-revise is a common LLM orchestration pattern for scaling inference-time compute. One LLM drafts, a second critiques the draft and provides feedback, and a third uses that feedback to revise the draft into the final output. As context cascades between stages, LLMs at different stages can resolve a context-dependent expression such as "previous" differently. When that happens, the expression undergoes a deictic shift, a change in what it refers to. This phenomenon was studied with a synthetic dataset of 10 base examples, each rendered in three conditions. Holding the shared components constant, the conditions varied whether the draft stage LLM (the assistant) or the verify stage LLM (the grader) resolved the expression correctly, and how much independent reasoning the revise stage LLM (the meta-evaluator) needed to determine which reading was correct. Six models from three providers were tested across 21 reasoning effort configurations using e-values for sequential testing, in a primary experiment and an ablation experiment that removed error classification labels from the grader's feedback. A separate LLM analyzed the meta-evaluator's stated rationale for each wrong verdict. Balanced accuracy (the unweighted mean of sensitivity and specificity) ranged from 0.156, below chance, to near-perfect. GPT-5.2 rose from 0.156 without reasoning to 0.942 at its highest reasoning effort level, while Gemini 3 Pro stayed above 0.94 at every level. Gemini 3 Pro at low reasoning effort outscored GPT-5.2 at xhigh reasoning effort for roughly 5% of the cost per trial. When the meta-evaluator erred, it tended to rely on surface cues rather than operational reasoning. Context engineers implementing draft-verify-revise pipelines should be wary of deictic shifts and make the intended referent explicit at each stage.

</details>


### [128] [GLARE: Generative Learning via Adversarial Reward Estimation For Social Dynamics Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12165)
*Tenghao Huang,Zhaoxuan Tan,Muhao Chen,Jonathan May,Mengting Wan,Longqi Yang,Pei Zhou,Sihao Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出会议动态预测基准MDFB与生成方法GLARE，用于评估和提升模型在多方长会议中预测后续对话、保持议程推进及角色一致性的能力。MDFB包含2,207场真实会议和24,794个面向未来的问题；GLARE通过对抗模仿学习、动态负样本和KL正则化策略奖励训练生成模型。结果显示，GLARE在效用和类人性上的人工评测胜率分别为0.66和0.70，优于SFT与SPIN，但仍低于真实人类续写。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对话生成评测通常依赖对真实后续文本的精确匹配，难以衡量开放式会议续写中更重要的能力，例如是否推进当前问题、是否遵循发言者角色、是否保持自然的多轮互动，以及能否处理意图和分歧。为此，论文希望建立一个面向真实会议动态的评测基准，并设计能够生成合理而非机械复现真实未来的训练方法。

Method: 论文构建Meeting Dynamic Forecasting Benchmark（MDFB），从2,207场真实会议中形成24,794个面向未来的查询。模型输入会议记录前缀和当前问题，一次生成可能的多轮后续对话。评测指标包括：1）效用，即续写对当前问题的推进程度；2）类人性，即对话流畅性和角色一致性。论文进一步提出GLARE，将对抗模仿学习适配到条件语言生成：判别器将真实续写与当前生成策略产生的样本进行区分，并将判别分数转化为带KL正则的策略奖励；训练过程中持续使用当前策略生成的负样本，使奖励函数随生成策略变化。

Result: GLARE在人工评测中的平均胜率为：效用0.66，类人性0.70，均优于监督微调（SFT）和SPIN，但仍未超过真实人类续写。论文还表明，MDFB可作为社会推理测试平台，通过参考辅助评判比较包括闭源系统在内的通用模型。

Conclusion: MDFB为会议后续行为提供了不要求精确复现真实文本的开放式评测框架，能够同时考察问题推进、对话自然性和角色保持。GLARE证明动态对抗模仿学习可以改善会议续写质量，但模型与人类续写之间仍存在差距。整体而言，该基准既适用于任务特定训练，也适用于基于输出的通用模型行为评估。

Abstract: Meeting continuation requires tracking the agenda, speaker roles, participant intentions, and disagreement across long multi-party discussions. We introduce the Meeting Dynamic Forecasting Benchmark (MDFB), constructed from 2,207 real-world meetings and 24,794 future-facing queries. Given a transcript prefix and an active question, a model generates a plausible multi-turn continuation in one call. We evaluate utility---progress toward the question---and human-likeness---plausible conversational flow and role consistency---without requiring exact reproduction of the observed future. We further present GLARE, an adaptation of adversarial imitation learning to conditional language generation. A discriminator ranks the observed continuation above samples from the current actor, and its score supplies a KL-regularized policy reward; retraining on current-policy negatives allows the reward landscape to evolve with the actor. GLARE attains average human-evaluated win rates of 0.66 on utility and 0.70 on human-likeness, outperforming SFT and SPIN while remaining below the observed human continuation. We also demonstrate MDFB as a social reasoning arena for comparing general-purpose models, including closed-source systems, through reference-assisted judgments. Together, these studies illustrate the benchmark's use for both task-specific learning and output-based evaluation of meeting behavior.

</details>


### [129] [WinSyn: An Automated Pipeline for Realistic Enterprise Question-Answering Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12171)
*Amey Varhade,Ananya Sutradhar,Ravishankar Krishnaswamy,Navin Goyal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个用于企业级问答与深度研究系统评测的自动化合成数据生成管线：模拟持续数月、最多25名员工参与的企业项目，生成相互关联的邮件、长短问题及基于数据的标准答案。实验表明，现有智能体基线在各数据集上的平均得分均低于80%，说明真实企业部署仍有明显改进空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业问答场景中的信息通常分散在邮件、聊天、文档等多种载体中，并且具有持续演化、信息歧义和内容冲突等特点。现有基准数据往往过于简单，问题偏短且不自然，难以真实反映企业环境中检索增强生成和深度研究系统面临的挑战，因此需要更复杂、更贴近实际的数据集与评测方法。

Method: 作者构建了一个自动化管线，用于生成反映真实职场场景的合成邮件数据，以及与数据内容对应的长答案问题、短答案问题和标准答案。该方法模拟持续数月的企业项目，设置多个岗位和最多25名相互协作的员工，并重点引入信息分散、歧义和自然产生的问题。随后，作者使用最新的前沿模型评测若干标准智能体基线。

Result: 在所生成的数据集上，所有数据集的查询平均得分均低于80%。这表明，即使采用较新的模型和智能体方法，面对复杂、分布式且存在歧义的企业信息时，系统仍存在明显性能瓶颈。

Conclusion: 该研究说明现有企业级深度研究和问答系统距离可靠部署仍有较大差距。高复杂度、现实感强的评测数据对于推动企业问答智能体的发展至关重要；所提出的生成管线为构建此类基准提供了可扩展方案。

Abstract: Enterprise settings provide a challenging environment for question-answering agents, which often rely on Retrieval-Augmented Generation, Deep Research (DR), and related techniques. Much of this challenge comes from the complexity of enterprise data: information is often spread across evolving and potentially conflict- ing emails, chat messages, documents, and other artifacts. Existing benchmarks typically have limited real-world complexity, short-form responses, and unnatural queries, so they often fail to capture the challenges of enterprise settings. In this work, we introduce an automated pipeline for generating synthetic datasets of emails reflecting realistic workplace scenarios, along with long- and short-form questions and gold answers grounded in the data. Our method simulates long-running enterprise projects spanning several months and involving up to 25 interacting employees across multiple roles. The data emphasizes ambiguity, distributed information, and naturally occurring queries. To validate the pipeline, we evaluate few standard agentic baselines on our datasets using the latest frontier models. We find that aggregate scores averaged over all queries remain below 80% for each dataset, indicating significant room for improvement. These findings suggest that more work remains to be done for enterprise deployment and underscore the importance of realistic, high-complexity evaluation data for developing stronger real-world enterprise DR systems.

</details>


### [130] [Soft Symbol Grounding for Prototypical Concepts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12247)
*Marcos Galván-López,Nijesh Upreti,Hiram Calvo,Carlos Aguilar-Ibáñez,Vaishak Belle*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Soft-PNet，一种统一的神经符号概念落地方法：利用每个概念一个标注锚点构建原型分布，并在可行符号解缓存上进行 Metropolis 随机游走，通过单一 KL 目标联合训练概念预测与符号解约束，从而减少推理捷径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经符号模型通常只获得最终标签监督，中间概念未被观测，因此同一标签可能对应多种概念分配，模型容易学会标签正确但概念错误的推理捷径。现有原型网络虽能缓解该问题，却依赖为不同任务手工设计的、连接感知与推理的可微损失，适用性和可迁移性受限。

Method: Soft-PNet 将概念落地重新表述为：在预先构建的可行符号解缓存上执行 Metropolis 随机游走，并使用每个概念一个标注锚点形成的原型分布引导采样。模型通过一个统一的 KL 散度目标，使原型加权后的符号解分布与网络输出的概念预测相匹配。该目标不依赖具体任务的手工损失；当解空间无法完全枚举时仍可使用。

Result: 在少量监督设置下的 MNIST-EvenOdd、Visual Sudoku 和 Kand-Logic 数据集上，Soft-PNet 在概念预测和最终标签预测层面均达到与依赖手工损失的原型网络相当的性能；相比软落地基线，它能够恢复更多正确概念，同时不需要任务专用损失，训练时间更短。

Conclusion: Soft-PNet 证明了通过原型分布、符号可行解缓存和统一 KL 训练目标，可以在不进行任务级损失工程的情况下缓解神经符号模型的推理捷径问题。该方法兼顾概念级正确性、标签级性能和训练效率，并具备跨任务及不可枚举解空间的适用潜力。

Abstract: Neuro-symbolic models are usually trained with supervision only on final labels, leaving the intermediate concepts unobserved. Since many concept assignments are consistent with a given label, training can predict labels correctly while recovering the wrong concepts, a failure known as a reasoning shortcut. Prototypical networks reduce shortcuts by anchoring each concept to a few labeled examples, but existing methods still couple perception and reasoning through a hand-crafted, task-specific differentiable loss that must be redesigned for every task. We introduce \textbf{Soft-PNet}, which removes this loss: it reframes concept grounding as a Metropolis walk over a precomputed cache of feasible symbolic solutions, guided by a prototype distribution built from a single labeled anchor per concept, and trains against one KL objective between the prototype-weighted cache and the network's concept predictions. The objective is identical across tasks and remains applicable when the solution space cannot be enumerated. On \texttt{MNIST-EvenOdd}, Visual Sudoku, and \texttt{Kand-Logic} under scarce supervision, Soft-PNet matches loss-engineered prototypical networks at the concept and label levels and recovers concepts that soft-grounding baselines miss, with no loss engineering and lower training time.

</details>


### [131] [GTA: Graph Theory Agent and Benchmark for Algorithmic Graph Reasoning with LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12265)
*Zixiang Xu,Yanbo Wang,Chenxi Wang,Lang Gao,Zirui Song,Yue Huang,Zhaorun Chen,Xiangliang Zhang,Xiuying Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 Graph Theory Bench（GT Bench）和 Graph Theory Agent（GTA），系统评估大语言模型在多种图表示和图算法任务上的推理能力，并通过表示选择与规划分解提升性能。GT Bench 包含24个经典图问题、44种任务结构、超过10万个样本，覆盖自然语言、结构化语言、邻接表和邻接矩阵四种表示。实验表明，输入表示对模型准确率影响显著，且最佳表示取决于图的密度、规模、拓扑结构和模型本身。GTA 在冻结执行模型外增加偏好训练的表示选择器及计划—分解机制，使 Phi-4 在简单集上的准确率从53.5%提升至69.1%，困难集从33.0%提升至41.5%，并能迁移到 GraCoRe 和 NLGraph。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型图推理评测存在三方面不足：任务通常过于简单且图规模较小；部分工作主要评价代码生成，而非模型直接基于图结构进行多步推理的能力；输入格式往往被固定，无法揭示表示方式对推理可靠性的影响。因此，论文旨在建立覆盖面更广、更系统的图理论推理基准，并研究不同图表示、图属性和模型之间的交互关系，进一步设计能够自动选择表示并组织推理过程的智能体。

Method: 论文首先构建 GT Bench，覆盖24个经典图问题和44种任务结构，生成超过100,000个样本，并采用自然语言、结构化语言、邻接表、邻接矩阵四种图表示。随后评估8个大语言模型，分析输入表示、图密度、图规模、拓扑结构和模型类型对准确率的影响。在此基础上提出 GTA：使用偏好训练的表示选择器，根据任务和图特征选择合适的输入表示；同时在冻结的执行模型周围加入计划与分解机制，以组织多步图算法推理。最后将其与8种提示和智能体基线比较，并在 GraCoRe 和 NLGraph 上进行迁移测试。

Result: 实验发现，模型准确率与图输入表示高度相关；不存在对所有情形都最优的统一表示，最佳表示会随图密度、规模、拓扑结构及模型变化。即使是能力最强的推理模型，这种表示敏感性仍然存在，但程度有所减弱。GTA 将 Phi-4 在 GT Bench 简单集上的准确率由53.5%提升至69.1%，在困难集上由33.0%提升至41.5%，超过8种提示和智能体基线；此外，GTA无需重新训练即可迁移到 GraCoRe 和 NLGraph。

Conclusion: 论文表明，大语言模型的图算法推理能力不仅取决于模型规模或推理能力，也显著取决于图结构的表达方式。图表示选择应被视为图推理系统的核心组成部分，而非固定的预处理步骤。通过自适应表示选择与计划—分解式推理，GTA能够有效提升多步图推理性能，并展现出跨基准迁移能力。该工作为评估和改进大语言模型的结构化数据推理提供了较系统的基准和方法。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly asked to reason over structured data such as graphs, yet how reliably they can carry out multi-step graph algorithms in language remains unclear. Existing evaluations tend to use simple tasks on small graphs, to score code generation rather than reasoning over the graph itself, or to fix a single input format. We introduce Graph Theory Bench (GT Bench), a benchmark covering 24 classical graph problems in 44 task-structure settings, with over 100,000 examples across four representations: natural language, structured language, adjacency list, and adjacency matrix. Evaluating eight LLMs on GT Bench shows that accuracy is strongly tied to the input representation, that the best representation shifts with graph density, size, and topology as well as with the model, and that this sensitivity persists, attenuated, in the strongest reasoning models. Building on these observations, we propose the Graph Theory Agent (GTA), which pairs a preference-trained representation selector with plan-and-decompose scaffolding around a frozen executor LLM. GTA lifts Phi-4 from 53.5% to 69.1% on the benchmark's easy split and from 33.0% to 41.5% on its hard split, outperforming eight prompting and agent baselines, and transfers without retraining to GraCoRe and NLGraph. Code for benchmark generation and evaluation: https://github.com/xzx34/GTA. The project homepage is available at https://xzx34.github.io/gta/.

</details>


### [132] [Learning Symbolic Constraint Representations from Examples: A Neuro-Symbolic Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12267)
*Nassim Belmecheri,Arnaud Gotlieb,Nadjib Lazaar,Helge Spieker*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种神经符号约束获取框架：用神经 Oracle Transformer 模拟用户回答并泛化概念知识，再由 FastCA 将其响应系统化地细化为可靠、 一致且可解释的约束网络，从而减少人工交互并实现自动模型构建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统约束获取方法依赖大量人工 Oracle 交互，导致时间成本高、查询次数多，难以高效学习用户定义的概念和约束。本文旨在降低用户参与度，同时保留符号约束模型的可靠性、可解释性与结构化特征。

Method: 框架由两个核心部分组成：1）训练神经 Oracle Transformer，使其基于已有示例模拟用户对查询的响应，并具备概念知识泛化能力；2）将学习到的 Oracle 接入 FastCA 约束获取引擎，由 FastCA 系统地校正和细化 Oracle 响应，最终生成 sound、consistent、interpretable 的约束网络。整体采用神经模式识别与符号推理相结合的神经符号方法。

Result: 实验结果表明，神经 Oracle 与 FastCA 的协同能够有效减少用户参与，并从数据中恢复结构化的符号模型。该方法在组合领域的自动模型构建中表现出较好的稳健性，并实现了数据驱动学习与符号推理的有效衔接。摘要未提供具体数据集、基线、指标或定量提升幅度。

Conclusion: 本文证明了神经符号交互可用于自动化约束获取：神经模型负责从示例中学习和泛化用户概念，符号引擎负责保证最终约束网络的正确性、一致性和可解释性。该框架为减少人工建模成本、在缺乏先验领域知识时构建组合问题模型提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Learning user-defined concepts as constraint networks has been extensively studied in the constraint acquisition (CA) literature. However, existing approaches typically rely on intensive interactions with a human oracle, making the learning process costly in terms of time and number of queries. In this paper, we propose a neuro-symbolic framework for automatic CA that significantly reduces user involvement by introducing neural Oracle Transformer models which learn to emulate user responses and to generalize conceptual knowledge. Trained on previously available examples, the learned oracle interacts with a dedicated CA engine, FastCA, which systematically refines the oracle's responses into a sound, consistent, and interpretable constraint network. This neuro-symbolic interaction enables the recovery of structured symbolic models from data without prior domain knowledge. Our results demonstrate that this neuro-symbolic interplay effectively aligns data-driven pattern recognition with symbolic reasoning, offering a robust approach to automating model construction in combinatorial domains.

</details>


### [133] [T-GADE: Thermodynamical Generative-AI-Driven Evolution of LLM Artifacts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12286)
*Kyoko Ogawa,Naoki Mori*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出T-GADE，将热力学遗传算法与大语言模型遗传算子及工件级多样性评估结合，用于演化“描述+代码”等结构化工件。在在线装箱任务上，生成式 Bose 型T-GADE显著降低训练阶段的相对额外箱数，并在迁移验证中达到与EoH相同的中位性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将进化计算与大语言模型结合时，既需要利用LLM的生成能力，也需要维持种群多样性。现有方法还缺乏统一的选择机制来处理重复基因型、结构化输出及训练到不同任务分布的迁移。因此，论文旨在构建一种兼顾探索、利用和多样性控制的LLM辅助进化框架。

Method: 提出T-GADE（Thermodynamical Genetic Algorithm for Artifact Design/Evolution），核心包括：1）使用LLM实现遗传操作，演化描述与代码组成的结构化工件；2）引入工件级多样性评价；3）采用统一自由能目标支持世代式和稳态式更新；4）通过Fermi型占据规则排除重复基因型，通过Bose型占据规则允许重复基因型；5）证明精确移除一个成员的性质，并给出恢复EoH零温生存规则的条件；6）在在线装箱问题上，以相对于体积下界的额外箱数作为训练和迁移评估指标。

Result: 在在线装箱实验中，生成式Bose型T-GADE、温度T=0.003时，20次运行的训练额外箱数中位数从1.152%降至0.815%，约降低29%；双侧Mann–Whitney检验p=0.042，Cliff’s δ=0.378，表明改进具有统计显著性但效应量为中等。对最终候选中排名最高的两个进行验证选择后，迁移额外箱数中位数达到0.496%，与EoH相同。

Conclusion: T-GADE表明，热力学选择机制能够有效协调LLM生成能力与进化种群多样性，并提升训练性能。保留多个候选并通过验证进行选择，有助于实现跨装箱容量的迁移。总体而言，论文验证了热力学选择和验证驱动的工件保留策略在LLM辅助进化中的实用价值，但实验目前主要基于单一在线装箱任务，方法的普适性仍需更多任务和更大规模实验验证。

Abstract: Integrating evolutionary computation and large language models (LLMs) requires control of population diversity as well as generative capability. Among LLM outputs, those with explicit structure, such as a description paired with code, are structured artifacts; we use artifact for short. We propose T-GADE, which evolves these artifacts by extending thermodynamical genetic algorithms through LLM-based genetic operators and artifact-level diversity evaluation. A common free-energy objective supports generational and steady-state updates, with Fermi-type occupancy excluding repeated genotypes and Bose-type occupancy permitting them. We establish exact one-member removal and conditions for recovering the zero-temperature survival rule of Evolution of Heuristics (EoH). On the online bin-packing task studied in the EoH paper, excess measures relative bin-count overhead above a volume lower bound. Training excess uses search instances; transfer excess uses instances with another bin capacity. Generational Bose-type T-GADE at $T=0.003$ reduced median training excess by approximately 29%, from 1.152% to 0.815%, over 20 runs per configuration (two-sided Mann-Whitney $p=0.042$, Cliff's $δ=0.378$). Validation selection among its two highest-ranked final candidates reached the same median transfer excess as EoH, 0.496%. These results demonstrate the utility of thermodynamical selection and validation-based use of retained artifacts.

</details>


### [134] [Robust Prototypical Networks for Few-Shot Sensor Fault Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12287)
*Mohammed Ayalew Belay,Amirshayan Haghipour,Pierluigi Salvo Rossi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出多回合原型网络（MEPN），通过聚合多个相互独立支持回合的类别原型并取均值，降低极低样本场景下的原型方差；在DeFACTO传感器数据集上进行五类故障分类实验，结果显示其单样本聚合性能显著优于单回合基线，而在相同10个样本预算下与10-shot ProtoNet无显著差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业故障诊断通常只有极少量带标签的故障样本。标准原型网络依赖小规模支持集计算类别原型，在极低样本条件下容易产生较大的估计波动，导致分类决策不稳定。因此，研究旨在不改变编码器结构的前提下，通过更稳定的原型估计提升few-shot故障诊断性能。

Method: 提出Multi-Episode Prototypical Networks（MEPN）。在多个相互独立的支持回合中分别计算类别原型，再对这些原型求均值，作为最终类别代表。实验采用DeFACTO传感器数据集，对真实工业测量注入偏置、漂移、尖峰和噪声故障，开展五类故障分类；重点比较每回合1-shot且聚合10个支持回合的MEPN与单回合基线，并在总支持样本数相同（10个）时与K=10的ProtoNet进行公平比较。

Result: 在100次独立实验中，MEPN在每回合K=1、聚合N_agg=10的设置下达到文中所示的SensorOneShotGcpn%性能，并显著优于单回合基线。相同10个支持样本预算下，MEPN与K=10的ProtoNet在统计上无显著差异，说明性能提升主要来自原型累积与方差降低，而非固定预算下更强的学习能力。摘要未给出SensorOneShotGcpn%的具体数值，因此无法进一步量化提升幅度。

Conclusion: MEPN能够通过跨支持回合聚合原型，缓解极低样本故障诊断中的原型不稳定问题，并在不修改编码器架构的情况下获得更可靠的分类表现。实验支持“原型累积降低估计方差”这一核心解释，但其优势本质上依赖于使用更多总支持样本；在相同样本预算下，它并未显著超越标准10-shot ProtoNet。

Abstract: Industrial fault diagnosis often operates with only a handful of labeled fault examples, making few-shot learning attractive for sensor monitoring. Standard prototypical networks are simple and effective; however, their class prototypes may become unstable in the very-low-shot regime because each decision relies on a small support set. We propose \emph{Multi-Episode Prototypical Networks} (MEPN), which aggregate prototypes from multiple disjoint support episodes and use their mean as the final class representative, reducing prototype variance without changing the encoder architecture. We evaluate MEPN on the DeFACTO sensor dataset using five-way fault classification with synthetic bias, drift, spike, and noise faults injected into real industrial measurements. Over 100 independent runs, MEPN reaches \textbf{\SensorOneShotGcpn\%} in the per-episode one-shot setting ($K\!=\!1$ shot, aggregated over $N_{\text{agg}}\!=\!10$ support episodes), substantially above single-episode baselines. Under an equal 10-sample support budget, MEPN and ProtoNet at $K\!=\!10$ are statistically indistinguishable, confirming prototype accumulation as the mechanism rather than superior fixed-budget learning.

</details>


### [135] [Hybrid Physics-AI Framework of Body Center of Mass Dynamics from Wrist-Worn Sensors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12304)
*Shuhao Que,Valentina Breschi,Ying Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种将腕部惯性测量单元（wrist-worn IMU）信号映射为人体质心（COM）加速度的简化运动学模型（KM），并进一步结合神经网络构建混合人工智能模型（HKM-NN），以提升在日常步态和坐站转换动作中的估计精度及噪声鲁棒性。KM在步态和坐站转换中的误差分别为6.7%–12.5%和5.6%；HKM-NN将误差进一步降低至5.3%–9.3%和最佳3.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 腕部IMU便于长期、非侵入式健康监测，但腕部运动不能充分代表全身动力学。人体质心（COM）被视为反映整体身体运动的重要生理参考，因此需要一种仅依赖腕部IMU、无需全身多传感器系统即可估计COM加速度的方法。同时，纯物理模型可能受简化假设限制，纯数据驱动模型又可能缺乏可解释性和噪声鲁棒性，促使作者探索生物力学模型与神经网络的融合。

Method: 作者首先建立简化运动学模型（KM），通过若干约化假设，使基于腕部IMU测量求解动力学方程成为可能，实现腕部信号到COM加速度的映射。在此基础上构建三类人类运动学模型驱动的神经网络（HKM-NN）：串行学习（ser-）以及两种同步学习方法（sim1-和sim2-），用于融合灰盒模型的结构先验与黑盒神经网络的数据拟合能力。模型使用10名健康志愿者在6种步态活动和坐站（SS）转换动作中的腕部IMU数据及COM真实测量值进行训练与测试，并在高斯扰动和椒盐噪声条件下评估鲁棒性。

Result: KM能够有效估计COM加速度：步态活动误差为6.7%–12.5%，坐站转换误差为5.6%。相较KM，HKM-NN模型显著降低误差，步态活动达到5.3%–9.3%，坐站转换最佳误差为3.9%。噪声实验显示，不同模型具有不同鲁棒性：sim1-和sim2-在高斯扰动下通常保持更强稳定性，而KM对椒盐噪声表现出相对更好的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 研究证明，仅利用腕部IMU估计人体COM加速度具有可行性。将简化生物力学结构与神经网络结合能够进一步提升估计精度，并在不同噪声条件下提供具有差异化的鲁棒性。该方法为低传感器数量、可穿戴、日常环境下的全身运动监测提供了有价值的技术路径。不过，摘要未报告跨受试者泛化、不同人群及真实长期生活场景中的性能，这些仍是后续验证重点。

Abstract: Wrist-worn IMU has been widely used for daily-life health monitoring. Yet, it does not fully represent whole-body dynamics, for which the body center of mass (COM) is considered the physiological reference standard. Therefore, this work proposes a simplified kinematic model (KM), which is designed to map the wrist IMU to the COM acceleration. It is built upon several reductive assumptions that enable the solvability of the dynamic equations based on wrist IMU measurements alone. This work further proposes three types of hybrid AI modeling methods, namely human kinematic model-based neural network (HKM-NN) models, to leverage the power of both grey-box and black-box modeling. The HKM-NN methods include serial learning (ser-) and two approaches of simultaneous learning (sim1- and sim2-). The proposed models are trained and tested using our dataset, which includes wrist IMU measurements and ground-truth COM measurements from 10 healthy volunteers during six gait activities and sit-to-stand (SS) transitional movement. The results demonstrate the feasibility of estimating COM acceleration from wrist IMU measurements. Our KM model yields satisfactory results, with an error ranging from 6.7% to 12.5% for gait activities and 5.6% for the SS. In comparison with the KM model, our HKM-NN models significantly enhance the performance, achieving 5.3% to 9.3% errors for gait activities, and the best error of 3.9% for the SS. In addition, the HKM-NN models demonstrate distinct robustness characteristics under noisy test conditions, with sim1-/sim2- generally maintaining greater robustness under Gaussian perturbations, while the KM model exhibits comparatively strong robustness under salt-and-pepper noise. These findings highlight the importance of combining biomechanical structure with data-driven learning for wearable sensing applications operating under imperfect and noisy measurement conditions.

</details>


### [136] [Do Influence-Derived Data Perturbations Enable Machine Unlearning? A Controlled Study of Three Plausible Roles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12313)
*Chenkai Wu,Chrispine Kambimbi,Qinyang Zeng,Jun Yan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文在统一评测协议和精确种子重训练基线下，系统检验深度扰动学习（DPL）在机器遗忘中的三种角色。审计发现公开实现存在图像预处理错配和标签扰动小于 float32 分辨率两项正确性问题。修正后，DPL 在 CIFAR-10/ResNet-18 上不能直接实现删除，效用影响跨种子不稳定，计入方向计算成本后也不优于简单热启动基线；Tiny ImageNet 的单种子结果同样不支持其作为正则器或热启动方案，但代码预处理不一致使直接删除比较无法定论。结论仅适用于随机实例删除。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有工作将 DPL 同时描述为直接删除信号、保持效用的正则器和对抗式遗忘的热启动方法，并以较弱角色的证据支持最强的直接删除主张。本文旨在拆分并独立检验这些角色，避免将不同目标或不匹配的实验结果混为一谈，同时审计公开实现的正确性。

Method: 在匹配的评测协议下，分别测试 DPL 的三种角色，并采用精确随机种子的重训练结果作为基线。首先审计公开代码，发现图像方向在增强、归一化张量上计算却施加于原始图像，且标签扰动小于 float32 的可分辨精度、实际未改变标签。随后修正图像扰动流程，在 CIFAR-10/ResNet-18 上进行三组配对种子实验，并将方向计算时间计入成本；另外在 Tiny ImageNet 上进行一次种子检查。

Result: 修正后，DPL 在 CIFAR-10/ResNet-18 的三个配对种子中均未满足直接删除标准。其对模型效用的影响在不同种子间正负不一致；计入方向计算时间后，表现逊于简单热启动基线。Tiny ImageNet 的单种子结果也不支持 DPL 作为效用正则器或热启动方法，但由于发布代码存在预处理不一致，直接比较结果无法得出确定结论。

Conclusion: 在本文覆盖的随机实例删除场景和实验条件下，没有证据支持 DPL 作为可靠的直接删除信号、效用保持正则器或高效热启动方法。研究同时强调，扰动式删除方法的评估应采用角色匹配的协议、精确重训练基线、正确的数值实现以及完整的计算成本核算。该结论不排除基于影响函数的方法在其他删除场景中的有效性。

Abstract: We evaluate Deep Perturbation Learning (DPL), which perturbs training images and labels along influence-derived directions, in three roles in which prior work has positioned it for machine unlearning: a direct deletion signal (the strongest claim), a utility-preserving regularizer, and a warm start for adversarial unlearning. Evidence for the weaker roles has been used to support the stronger one, so we test each role separately under a matched protocol with exact-seed retraining baselines. An audit of the public implementation identifies two correctness issues: image directions are computed on augmented, normalized tensors but applied to raw images, and the label perturbation falls below float32 resolution, leaving labels unchanged. After correcting the image-perturbation pipeline, DPL fails the direct-deletion criterion on CIFAR-10/ResNet-18 in all three paired seeds. Its utility effects are inconsistent in sign across seeds, and once direction-computation time is counted it underperforms simple warm-start baselines. A one-seed Tiny ImageNet check likewise does not favor DPL as a regularizer or warm start; preprocessing inconsistencies in the released code make the direct comparison there inconclusive. These results cover random instance deletion only and do not rule out influence-based methods in other deletion regimes. We release a role-matched evaluation protocol and an audit checklist for perturbation-based deletion claims.

</details>


### [137] [AIM: A Privacy-Aware Interoperable Memory Framework for Multi-Agent Multi-User LLM Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12320)
*Zachary Johnson,Nigel Boachie Kumankumah,Somya Chatterjee,Tejas Sathyamurthi,Min Chen,Xinyi Alice Li,Xiao Wang,Emily Morgan Gelchie,Jessica Lin,Sadid A. Hasan,Sulaiman Vesal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AIM（Agentic Interoperable Memory），一种面向多智能体、多用户大语言模型系统的隐私感知记忆框架，并发布MUMBench基准数据集。AIM将记忆动态划分为用户私有记忆和可共享公共记忆，通过索引级访问控制保护隐私，同时促进跨用户知识共享。在MUMBench上，AIM取得96.0%的可见性分类准确率、58.8%的严格操作准确率和70.5%的状态感知操作准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统大语言模型的记忆通常局限于单次用户会话，无法持续学习用户偏好。已有智能体记忆系统主要面向单个用户，虽然能实现长期记忆，却难以在多个用户之间共享有益的公共知识，从而限制多智能体协作、响应一致性和系统整体性能。与此同时，跨用户共享记忆又带来隐私泄露风险。因此，研究需要一种能够同时管理私有记忆与公共记忆，并实施细粒度访问控制的统一框架；还需要一个专门评估多用户记忆操作的数据集。

Method: 作者提出AIM框架，核心是对信息进行动态可见性分类：私有信息仅归属于特定用户，其他用户不可访问；公共信息则可被所有用户使用。框架在索引层面实施访问控制，确保检索阶段遵循记忆的权限边界。除记忆检索外，AIM还支持记忆的创建、更新和删除。作者进一步构建MUMBench，这是一个包含四个领域、多用户交互以及私有和可共享信息的数据集，用于系统评估多用户环境中的多种记忆操作，并采用严格操作准确率和状态感知操作准确率等指标。

Result: 在MUMBench上的三次独立实验中，AIM取得96.0%的可见性分类准确率，说明其能够较准确地区分私有信息和公共信息；严格操作准确率为58.8%，表示同时满足操作类型、内容及状态要求的比例仍有提升空间；状态感知操作准确率为70.5%，表明在结合当前记忆状态判断创建、更新、删除或检索操作时，系统表现有所改善。

Conclusion: AIM验证了在多用户、多智能体LLM系统中统一管理私有记忆和共享记忆的可行性，并在保护用户隐私的同时利用公共知识提升协作和响应一致性。MUMBench填补了多用户记忆操作评测数据集的空白。不过，严格操作准确率相对有限，说明复杂的记忆状态管理、操作决策和长期一致性仍是后续研究重点。

Abstract: Traditional large language models (LLMs) are scoped to individual user sessions, limiting their knowledge to a single conversation and preventing them from learning user preferences that evolve over time. Existing agentic memory systems address this limitation but generally operate at the individual-user level, restricting the public knowledge that could be shared across users to improve downstream responses. We introduce AIM (Agentic Interoperable Memory), a unified, privacy-aware memory framework that enables multi-agent, multi-user LLM systems to persistently manage private and shared memory. AIM dynamically classifies information as private, scoped to one user and inaccessible to others, or public, accessible to all users. It enforces index-level access controls so that private memories are retrievable only by their owner, protecting sensitive data while allowing beneficial shared knowledge to improve coordination and consistency. We also introduce MUMBench (Multi-User Memory Benchmark), a dataset of multi-user interactions containing private and shareable information across four domains. To our knowledge, MUMBench is the first public dataset designed to evaluate multiple memory operations, including retrieval, creation, update, and deletion, in a multi-user environment. Across three independent runs on MUMBench, AIM achieves 96.0% visibility classification accuracy, 58.8% strict operation accuracy, and 70.5% state-aware operation accuracy.

</details>


### [138] [Affective Agent: On-Device Personalized Intervention Reasoning for Wearable Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12322)
*Reina Mun,Zishen Wan,Vijay Janapa Reddi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Affective Agent，一种面向可穿戴设备的三层参考架构，利用紧凑型语言模型、生理信号、环境上下文和用户历史，在无需云端依赖或个体化再训练的情况下，判断是否、何时以及如何实施个性化干预。系统通过主机管理的结构化记忆演化实现个性化，并在室内环境质量控制的合成纵向场景中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有情感计算和可穿戴状态推断主要关注识别用户状态，但在设备端进一步完成干预决策仍面临困难，包括不确定性处理、个体差异、上下文依赖、信号质量变化以及有限的设备计算资源。本文旨在将可穿戴系统从状态感知推进到闭环、个性化的设备端干预，同时避免云端依赖和针对每位用户的模型参数更新。

Method: 提出由感知层、个性化层和推理层组成的三层架构。系统将亚十亿参数规模的紧凑型语言模型与生理证据、环境上下文及用户历史相结合，进行是否干预、干预时机和干预方式的决策。个性化不通过用户级权重微调实现，而是由主机管理结构化记忆的持续演化实现。作者以室内环境质量控制为应用实例，并使用模拟器生成的、留出的纵向场景进行评估；场景覆盖生理变化、上下文差异、信号质量和既往干预历史。系统还采用两遍式结构化推理，以增强决策过程。

Result: 在合成评估中，基于记忆的个性化机制和两遍式结构化推理均改善了干预决策表现。结果表明，紧凑型语言模型能够在可穿戴级硬件上结合多源信息进行设备端干预推理。摘要未提供具体数据集规模、评价指标、基线模型或性能提升幅度，因此无法据此判断改进的统计显著性和实际效果大小。

Conclusion: 本文展示了一条将可穿戴状态推断扩展为设备端闭环个性化干预的可行路径。其核心贡献是将轻量语言模型、多源证据、结构化用户记忆和分层推理结合起来，在不依赖云端及用户级再训练的条件下实现干预决策。不过，当前结论主要基于模拟生成的室内环境质量场景，真实用户实验、跨场景泛化、能耗延迟、安全性以及长期个性化稳定性仍需进一步验证。

Abstract: Affective computing has advanced wearable state inference, but on-device reasoning about whether, when, and how to intervene remains challenging. We present Affective Agent, a three-layer reference architecture for personalized intervention reasoning under uncertainty on wearable-class hardware. It combines a compact sub-billion-parameter language model with physiological evidence, context, and user history to decide whether, when, and how to intervene, without cloud dependency or per-user retraining. The architecture is organized into three interacting layers (perception, personalization, and reasoning), adapting to individual users through host-managed structured memory evolution rather than per-user weight updates. We instantiate Affective Agent in indoor environmental quality control and evaluate it on held-out, simulator-generated longitudinal scenarios spanning physiological variation, context, signal quality, and intervention history. Results show that memory-driven personalization and two-pass structured reasoning improve intervention decisions within this synthetic evaluation. By moving the decision layer on-device, this work demonstrates a path from wearable state inference toward closed-loop, personalized intervention on wearable-class hardware.

</details>


### [139] [LoRA-RC: Reservoir Computing with Low-Rank Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12327)
*Wenbin Wan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 LoRA-RC：通过低秩递归矩阵修正和在线误差驱动适应，使储备池计算能够应对系统漂移，同时保持收缩性与增量输入到状态稳定性。实验表明，在 Lorenz 系统突变漂移下，LoRA-RC 相比固定 RC 和仅读出层适应分别降低漂移后预测误差 56% 和 51%；移除投影后误差扩大超过 40 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统储备池计算仅训练线性读出层，虽然训练快速、数据效率高，但在目标系统发生漂移时，固定储备池难以持续适应，而仅更新读出层可能不足。直接无约束地适应递归矩阵又可能破坏回声状态性质和增量稳定性。因此，论文旨在设计一种能够在线适应系统变化、同时保留储备池可靠稳定性的机制。

Method: 提出 LoRA-RC，在固定的基础储备池和固定适应基底上，对递归矩阵施加低秩修正。在线阶段利用流式预测误差更新一个小规模核心矩阵；每次更新后将其投影到谱范数球，并进行低通滤波。谱范数投影确保实际应用的递归矩阵始终位于经过认证的收缩集合内。理论上，论文沿每条在线适应路径建立储备池的增量输入到状态稳定性界，并证明其收敛速率和增益与具体适应路径无关。

Result: 在具有突发参数漂移的 Lorenz 系统上，LoRA-RC 的漂移后预测误差相比固定 RC 降低 56%，相比仅进行读出层适应降低 51%。基于 20 个随机种子的消融实验显示，移除谱范数投影会使漂移后预测误差扩大 40 倍以上，说明稳定性约束对方法性能至关重要。

Conclusion: LoRA-RC 在保持储备池收缩性、回声状态性质和增量稳定性保障的同时，实现了对系统漂移的高效在线适应。结果表明，低秩参数化降低了在线更新成本，谱范数投影则是防止适应过程破坏稳定性的关键组成部分。

Abstract: Reservoir computing (RC) trains only a linear readout over a fixed recurrent layer, making it fast and data-efficient for online prediction. However, a static reservoir degrades under system drift, readout-only adaptation is then insufficient, and unconstrained reservoir adaptation can destroy the echo-state and incremental stability properties that make RC reliable. This paper proposes LoRA-RC, which adapts the recurrent matrix through a low-rank correction driven by streaming prediction errors. The base reservoir and adaptation bases are fixed offline; a small core matrix is adapted online, projected onto a spectral-norm ball, and low-pass filtered at each step. The projection guarantees that every applied recurrent matrix remains within a certified contraction set, and an incremental input-to-state stability bound is established for the reservoir along each online adaptation path, with path-independent rate and gain. On a Lorenz system with an abrupt parameter drift, LoRA-RC cuts post-drift prediction error by 56% versus a fixed RC and 51% versus readout-only adaptation; ablations over 20 seeds show that removing the projection inflates this error by more than a factor of 40.

</details>


### [140] [Toward Robust Personalized Alignment for LLMs: Mitigating Persona Drift in Multi-Turn Dialogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12373)
*Youyuan Zhang,Siyuan Li,Fangming Liu,Jing Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 CORE，一种面向长期交互的个性漂移控制方法，并构建 PERSIST 基准评估模型在连续交互压力下的持久人格状态鲁棒性。实验表明，CORE 能提升个性化对齐、鲁棒性和规范化闭槽状态保真度，且收益来自显式的更新控制，而非单纯增强生成能力或记忆机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有个性化语言模型通常将用户画像视为固定状态，难以处理用户偏好在长期交互中的真实变化。模型既需要在偏好确实改变时更新持久人格状态，也需要避免因短暂、含糊或尚未解决的信息而错误更新，从而产生 persona drift。现有评测对连续交互中的歧义、冲突及社会影响压力覆盖不足，因此需要新的状态更新机制和更严格的鲁棒性基准。

Method: 提出 CORE，将当前轮次的局部证据与持久人格状态修订过程分离，并通过不确定性感知的信念修订机制，有选择地更新具有依据的用户偏好。进一步提出 PERSIST，即 post-anchor 留出式基准，用于在顺序交互压力下评估人格状态鲁棒性，涵盖歧义、冲突和受控社会影响等情形。实验在 ALOE、PersonaChat 和 PERSIST 上进行，并结合人工评估与机制控制实验，区分显式更新控制与更强生成能力或单纯持久记忆的作用。

Result: CORE 在 ALOE、PersonaChat 和 PERSIST 上均提升了个性化对齐能力与鲁棒性，并在规范化闭槽状态保真度方面取得互补性提升。人工评估和机制控制结果进一步表明，性能改善主要支持显式的状态更新控制，而不能仅归因于更强的文本生成能力或持久记忆。摘要未提供具体数值、显著性检验或各数据集上的单独提升幅度。

Conclusion: 个性化语言模型需要对用户状态进行受控、基于证据且不确定性感知的动态修订，而不是简单地持续累积记忆。CORE 为缓解 persona drift 提供了有效方案，PERSIST 则补充了面向长期顺序交互的鲁棒性评测。总体结论是，显式控制何时以及如何更新人格状态，是维持长期个性化质量的关键。

Abstract: Persona drift remains a central challenge for personalized language models, as user profiles evolve over long interactions rather than remain permanently fixed. Models must therefore revise persistent persona states when preferences genuinely change, while avoiding updates driven by transient, ambiguous, or unresolved observations. We propose CORE, which separates turn-local evidence from persistent persona-state revision and selectively updates grounded user preferences through uncertainty-aware belief revision. We also introduce PERSIST, a held-out post-anchor benchmark for persona-state robustness under sequential interaction stress, covering ambiguity, conflict, and controlled social influence. Across ALOE, PersonaChat, and PERSIST, CORE improves personalized alignment and robustness, with complementary gains in normalized closed-slot state fidelity. Human evaluation and mechanistic controls further support explicit update control beyond stronger generation or persistent memory alone.

</details>


### [141] [BlueLM-GUI Technical Report: A Real-Device-Centric Flywheel for Self-Improving Mobile GUI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12394)
*Tong Ye,Kunyang Han,Guozhi Wang,Longqiang Luo,Zhifeng Ding,Yongxiang Zhang,Xiaolei Shen,Yuxuan Zhang,Zhuping Zhang,Tao Xu,Yue Pan,Yucheng Zhao,Yupei Hu,Yuanjiang Ouyang,Danfeng Shen,Runqi Lin,Hongda Cai,Zhaoxiong Wang,Mengjia Yan,Yingjie Zhong,Chen Zhou,Zeyu Zhang,Xuwen Zhu,Penggang Shi,Mingcheng Luo,Ziyang Wu,Min Jin,Mingfu Shen,Zairong Xu,Fan Zhang,Hao Wang,Liang Liu,Zhulin Xie,Lijun Yao,Xiao Liang,Liangmin Wen,Liqiang Feng,Feilong Wu,Min Hu,Min Chen,Guanjing Xiong,Xiaohu Ruan,Xiaoxin Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 BlueLM-GUI，一种 35B-A3B 的端到端移动 GUI 智能体，通过真实设备训练、全轨迹利用和动态基准测试，解决训练分布偏移、真实设备失败样本浪费及固定基准饱和三大问题。在 MobileGUI-VBench 和 AndroidWorld 上取得领先或有竞争力的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有移动 GUI 智能体从多模块系统转向端到端模型，但工业部署仍面临三项关键挑战：沙箱训练环境与生产环境之间存在分布不匹配；真实设备上的高成本失败轨迹未被充分利用；静态基准测试容易饱和，难以持续指导模型迭代。因此，论文旨在构建一个以真实设备为核心、能够持续产生数据并推动模型改进的训练与评测闭环。

Method: 论文提出 BlueLM-GUI（35B-A3B），采用由三个原则构成的真实设备驱动闭环。1）Every Sample Matters：建立双轨数据处理流程，通过异构三系统共识评估（Heterogeneous Triple-System Consensus）筛选和判断轨迹，并利用错误纠正与推导模块（Error Correction & Derivation Module）将成功与失败轨迹转化为可用监督信号。2）Every Rollout Is Real：采用持续预训练、监督微调和智能体强化学习三阶段训练流程，训练与 rollout 均基于数百部真实手机，以减少模拟环境和部署环境之间的差异。3）Every Query Evolves：提出配额驱动的基准测试方法，从三个正交维度组织查询，实现性能归因，并在模型能力提升后系统升级测试集。

Result: BlueLM-GUI 在 MobileGUI-VBench 上取得 87.4 分，比最佳闭源模型高 5.1 分；在 AndroidWorld 上取得 84.9 分，达到开源模型最佳结果，并与闭源模型具有竞争力。结果表明，真实设备训练、轨迹充分利用和动态评测共同提升了移动 GUI 操作能力。

Conclusion: 论文表明，将模型训练和迭代评估同时锚定在真实设备环境，并贯彻“每个样本都利用、每次 rollout 都真实、每个查询都演化”三项原则，可以有效提升移动 GUI 智能体的性能、稳健性与跨环境迁移能力。其核心贡献不仅是模型性能提升，更在于构建了一个面向工业部署的持续数据—训练—评测飞轮。

Abstract: Mobile GUI agents are shifting from multi-module frameworks to native models trained end-to-end, yet industrial deployment faces three persistent gaps. Sandbox training produces a distribution mismatch with production environments; expensive real-device failures remain underutilized; and fixed benchmarks saturate, losing the power to guide iteration. We present BlueLM-GUI, a 35B-A3B mobile GUI agent built as a real-device-centric flywheel that closes these gaps through three principles. Every Sample Matters: a dual-track pipeline with Heterogeneous Triple-System Consensus evaluation and an Error Correction \& Derivation Module salvages every trajectory into usable supervision. Every Rollout Is Real: a three-stage recipe---continual pre-training, supervised fine-tuning, and agentic reinforcement learning on hundreds of real phones---grounds every rollout in real production environments, so the capability the model learns transfers directly to deployment. Every Query Evolves: a quota-driven benchmark methodology with three orthogonal axes enables precise attribution and allows the benchmark to be systematically upgraded as the model improves. BlueLM-GUI achieves 87.4 on MobileGUI-VBench, surpassing the best closed-source model by 5.1 points, and 84.9 on AndroidWorld, the best result among open-source models and competitive with closed-source models. These results demonstrate that grounding model training and iterative improvement in both real devices and the three Every principles yields strong, robust, and transferable mobile GUI capability.

</details>


### [142] [Is Gaussian Splatting Becoming Neural Again? A Taxonomy and Controlled Study of Learned Parameterization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12395)
*YuanHang Wang,Xin Cao,Yi Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统分析神经网络增强三维高斯泼溅（3DGS）的五类设计维度，并通过受控实验比较不同神经参数化方式。结果表明，应选择性地神经化：共享外观与不透明度有助于提升重建质量，但神经解码几何结构并未带来额外收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3DGS方法越来越多地使用神经网络生成或共享高斯参数，但不同方法的神经化位置和作用机制缺乏统一刻画。本文旨在建立系统分类框架，厘清各种设计分别解决哪些局限，并判断哪些参数适合由神经网络建模。

Method: 作者沿五个维度分析19种代表性方法：属性解码、空间共享、视角条件解码、拓扑生成和摊销推理。随后在Mip-NeRF 360数据集上进行受控实验，分别研究三种神经参数化形式，比较共享外观与不透明度、以及解码几何结构等方案对重建性能的影响。

Result: 分析显示，五类设计对应不同问题，不能简单归纳为“是否使用神经网络”。受控实验表明，共享外观和不透明度能够改善重建质量；而对几何结构进行神经解码未产生进一步收益。

Conclusion: 3DGS更适合采用选择性神经化策略：利用共享函数捕捉可复用的参数相关性，同时保留显式高斯泼溅在局部几何表达上的自由度。神经网络不应无差别地替代所有显式参数。

Abstract: Three-dimensional Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) combines explicit primitives with efficient rasterization, yet recent systems increasingly use neural networks to generate or share Gaussian parameters. We characterize this trend along five axes: attribute decoding, spatial sharing, view-conditioned decoding, topology generation, and amortized inference. An analysis of 19 representative methods shows that these choices address different limitations and cannot be reduced to a binary neural label. We also isolate three forms of neural parameterization in a controlled mip-NeRF 360 study. Sharing appearance and opacity improves reconstruction quality, while decoding geometric structure offers no further gain. The evidence favors selective neuralization: shared functions help when they capture reusable correlations without sacrificing the local geometric freedom of explicit splats.

</details>


### [143] [Niching Agents in The Core](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12398)
*Gary B. Parker,Jim O'Connor,John Asaro*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究一种无需传统适应度函数的竞争性协同进化算法 The Core，并通过环境分域（niching）提升控制器在特定子环境中的进化表现。实验结果表明，针对特定子环境进行分域的智能体优于面向整个环境或其他子环境分域的智能体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统进化算法通常依赖预先定义的适应度函数，但在复杂环境中设计有效且全面的适应度函数较为困难。The Core通过智能体间的局部竞争与协同进化实现自主控制，已有研究已证明其能够在Xpilot游戏中完成战斗和导航任务。本研究进一步探讨：将智能体限制在原始环境的特定子集内进行进化，是否能够提升其在对应子环境中的适应性和成功率。
