Practically, clinicians elicit evidence interactively and patient communication varies widely. We introduce MedRoundsQA, a multi-turn diagnostic benchmark derived from 1,387 board-exam cases across 17 specialties. Each case is converted into a structured 24-slot clinical record, and then instantiated as controlled doctor-patient dual-agent dialogues under varying patient personas, with the underlying clinical content held fixed. We further classify cases by difficulty using model-based uncertainty to enable easy-to-hard analysis. Evaluations of fifteen LLM doctor agents show that (i) moving from a single-turn diagnosis on the standardized records to multi-turn consultations causes large degradations of roughly 13-39 points; (ii) more turns reliably improves question relevance, but diagnostic accuracy exhibits diminishing returns and typically plateaus after 6-12 turns; and (iii) patient persona differences can shift diagnosis accuracy by about 7-8 points (lowest to highest education), highlighting equity risks that single-turn benchmarks miss.

</details>


### [178] [Tracing and Coordinating Cross-Layer Influence for Multimodal Model Merging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12897)
*Pengyang Zhou,Xiaobin Tu,Zhengxi Liu,Rongkun Xue,Haochen Li,Miancan Liu,Ziyuan Chen,Yinggui Wang,Jinkui Ren,Xiantao Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 TAC-Merge，一种面向多模态模型合并的方法，通过追踪跨层视觉与语言专家更新的耦合影响，并联合优化区域权重，将多个任务专家整合为一个兼具互补能力且具备未知任务泛化能力的共享模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单模态及多模态模型合并方法通常在各层内独立组合专家更新，忽略了更新会改变后续层输入表示，并进一步影响视觉—语言交互。因此，不同模态更新之间存在跨层传播与耦合效应，带来两个关键问题：如何刻画单个专家更新在深度方向上的多模态影响，以及如何依据这些影响联合组合专家更新。

Method: 提出 TAC-Merge，包含两个模块。第一，多模态影响映射（MIM）构建专家更新效果图，并结合 Ricci 曲率与专家预测结果定义共享融合目标，以刻画更新在跨层和跨模态路径上的影响。第二，耦合合并控制（CMC）建模不同系数调整之间的交互关系，并联合优化区域权重，从而合成一个共享模型。

Result: 在多种多模态任务上的实验表明，TAC-Merge 能够有效整合不同专家的互补能力，并在未见任务上展现出良好的泛化性能。摘要未提供具体数据集、基线、指标或数值提升。

Conclusion: 跨层多模态影响和专家更新之间的耦合关系是模型合并中的重要因素。通过 MIM 进行影响建模、通过 CMC 进行联合权重控制，TAC-Merge 可比传统逐层独立合并更有效地构建统一多模态模型，并提升其任务迁移与泛化能力。

Abstract: Multimodal model merging aims to consolidate task experts into a single model that retains their complementary capabilities. Most unimodal model merging methods combine expert updates within individual layers, and multimodal approaches largely follow this design. However, an expert update changes the representations passed to subsequent layers, allowing its influence to propagate across depth and affect how visual and textual information interact. When visual and language updates are combined, later updates act on inputs already modified by earlier ones, coupling their effects. This poses two challenges: (1) how to characterize the multimodal influence of individual expert updates across depth, and (2) how to jointly combine expert updates based on their multimodal influence. To address these challenges, we propose TAC-Merge for tracing and coordinating cross-layer influence in multimodal model merging. It contains two modules, i.e., multimodal influence mapping (MIM) and coupled merge control (CMC). MIM constructs graphs of update effects and uses Ricci curvature together with expert predictions to define a shared fusion objective. CMC models interactions among coefficient adjustments and jointly optimizes regional weights to synthesize one shared model. Experiments across diverse multimodal tasks demonstrate the effectiveness of TAC-Merge in consolidating complementary expert capabilities and supporting generalization to unseen tasks.

</details>


### [179] [EduFair-Bench: Evaluating Pedagogical Fairness of LLM Tutors Across Student Demographics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12949)
*Jiaxu Zhao,Bahar Radmehr,Fares Fawzi,Tanya Nazaretsky,Tanja Käser*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 EduFair-Bench，用于系统审计大语言模型作为智能导师时的教学公平性。研究发现，模型能力与人口统计公平性基本相互独立；更强模型并不必然更公平，语言和移民背景相关线索造成的差异大于性别和社会经济地位相关线索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型被广泛用于辅导学习，现有评估主要关注总体正确性和教学质量，尚不清楚不同人口统计背景的学生是否能获得同等质量的辅导。本文旨在建立可控、可量化的基准，识别辅导质量是否随学生性别、移民背景、第一语言和社会经济地位而系统变化，并区分偏差来自导师模型还是学生特征线索。

Method: 构建 EduFair-Bench，将数学、物理和化学多领域题库与受控交互模拟结合。使用固定的语言模型学生，分别让其与五个导师模型交互，并覆盖四个维度、九种人口统计水平。教学质量通过五项逐轮教学指标和四项对话级指标评估；评估采用经180个导师回合、三名标注者共识验证的语言模型裁判。使用配对 Wilcoxon 符号秩检验和自助法效应量置信区间衡量偏差。此外，通过姓名传递人口统计线索，以及导师与学生人口统计信息冲突两种消融实验，区分导师驱动偏差与学生驱动偏差。

Result: 在五个导师模型中，模型能力与人口统计公平性基本正交。最小模型表现出最强的一致性，而四个能力更强的导师均出现较大的群体差距，且不存在明确的能力—公平性排序。面向教学的强化学习训练并未消除偏差，而是重新分配了偏差。语言和移民背景相关线索引发的差距大于性别和社会经济地位相关线索。

Conclusion: LLM导师的高能力不能作为教学公平性的代理指标，公平性必须被单独测量和审计。EduFair-Bench表明，教学强化学习可能改变偏差分布而非根除偏差；未来应重点研究语言与移民背景相关差异的机制，并结合更稳健的多群体评估、偏差缓解和人工验证来提升教育应用的公平性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed as tutors, but it is unclear whether they support all students equally well. We introduce \textbf{EduFair-Bench}, a benchmark for auditing the pedagogical fairness of LLM tutors---whether tutoring quality varies systematically with student demographics. EduFair-Bench pairs a multi-domain question bank (mathematics, physics, chemistry) with a controlled simulation in which a fixed LLM student interacts with each tutor across nine demographic levels spanning four dimensions: gender, immigration background, first language, and socioeconomic status (SES). Tutoring quality is scored on five turn-level pedagogical metrics and four conversation-level dimensions, using an LLM judge validated against three-annotator consensus on 180 tutor turns. Bias is measured via paired Wilcoxon signed-rank tests and bootstrap effect-size confidence intervals. Two ablations (demographic cues conveyed through names; conflicting demographic information between tutor and student) disentangle tutor-driven from student-driven bias. Across five tutors, we find that model capability and demographic fairness are largely orthogonal: the smallest model is the most consistent while the four more capable tutors all exhibit wide demographic gaps with no clear capability-to-fairness ordering, pedagogy-specific RL training redistributes rather than removes bias, and language- and immigration-related cues produce larger gaps than gender- and SES-related cues.

</details>


### [180] [How Good Are Frontier Models at Physics? Expert Re-Grading Reveals Broken Evaluations and Near-Saturation of Leading Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13009)
*Ali Ansari,Haoran Sun,Andy Zeyi Liu,Mark Jabbour,Yongshan Ding,Steven Girvin,Yu He,Sohrab Ismail-Beigi,Aleksander Kubica,Owen D. Miller,Corey O'Hern,Vidvuds Ozolins,David Poland,A. Douglas Stone,Frank C. van den Bosch,Logan Wright,Navid Akbari,Santanu Antu,Kangle Cai,Andrew Calabrese-Day,Mateo Cárdenes Wuttig,Meng Cheng,Barry T. Chiang,Ali Ghorashi,Shouzhen Gu,Haoyang Huang,Zhibo Kang,Lukas Kienesberger,Hantian Liu,Charles Lomba,Zhongling Lu,Wenchao Ma,Rohin E. McIntosh,Evan McKinney,Ivan Rojkov,Xulei Sun,Yarone Meir Tokayer,Naveen Balaji Umasankar,Mira Varma,Leda Wang,Qimin Wang,Tyler Wang,Haoyu Wei,Jinming Yang,Jinchen Zhao,Sherlock Tingrui Zhao,Qinyuan Zheng,Jay S. Zou,Lucas Baker,Arman Cohan,John Sous*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文审计六个物理学基准，发现大量低分源于评分器、参考答案或题目本身的问题；经专家修正后，前沿模型在规范物理题上的表现显著提升，说明现有基准低估了模型能力，并需要更严格的专家验证评测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有物理学基准显示前沿语言模型得分较低，但这一结论与领域专家的实际使用体验不一致。研究旨在判断低分究竟反映模型物理推理能力不足，还是来自错误参考答案、评分错误及题目歧义等基准质量问题。

Method: 在六个常用物理学基准上评测前沿模型，聚焦具有可验证最终答案的文本题。按物理子领域邀请相关 faculty 和研究生专家，逐项审查题目、参考解答和模型回答，区分真实模型错误与评分、答案或题目缺陷；随后修正错误参考答案，并修复或剔除有缺陷的问题，在保留题目子集上重新计算 mean@4 和 pass@4。

Result: 专家审计表明，许多最初被判为错误的案例实际上源于基准问题。GPT-5.6-Sol 在 HLE-Physics 上的 mean@4 从47.3%升至78.7%，在 CMT-Benchmark 上从61.0%升至87.2%；在保留的54个 CritPt挑战题上，修正后的 pass@4 达到94.4%。UGPhysics、PRISM-Physics 和 PHYBench 的审计子集得分也显著提高。

Conclusion: 现有物理学基准可能系统性低估前沿模型解决规范物理问题的能力。封闭式题目上的近饱和表现表明，未来评测应采用更困难、开放性更强且经过领域专家严格验证的任务。

Abstract: Low reported scores on leading physics benchmarks, including those featured in the Artificial Analysis Intelligence Index (2026), suggest that frontier language models still struggle with advanced physics, a demanding test of their scientific reasoning and quantitative problem-solving abilities. Yet this impression does not always align with domain experts' experiences using these models in their work. We revisit these reported findings by evaluating frontier models on six widely used physics benchmarks and auditing them with experts, focusing on text-only problems with verifiable final answers. For each subfield of physics, faculty and graduate researchers with relevant expertise carefully review problem statements, reference solutions, and model responses to distinguish genuine model errors from grader errors, incorrect reference solutions, and ambiguous or underspecified questions. Most audited cases initially evaluated as incorrect reflect these benchmarking issues rather than errors in the models' physics reasoning. We then ask experts to address these benchmarking issues by correcting erroneous reference solutions and repairing or excluding flawed questions. We find that GPT-5.6-Sol's measured mean@4 rises from 47.3% to 78.7% on HLE-Physics and from 61.0% to 87.2% on CMT-Benchmark, while its corrected pass@4 reaches 94.4% on the 54 retained CritPt challenges. Corrected scores are computed on the retained evaluation subsets following expert review. Scores on the audited subsets of UGPhysics, PRISM-Physics, and PHYBench also rise substantially after correction. These findings suggest that current benchmarks substantially understate frontier models' ability to solve well-posed physics problems. Near-saturation on these closed-ended tasks highlights the need for more demanding, expert-validated evaluations.

</details>


### [181] [Diffusion Models and Concept Formation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13047)
*Zekun Wang,Karthik Singaravadivelan,Christopher J. MacLellan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出：扩散模型虽主要用于图像生成，但其逐噪声层级的概率结构与Cobweb概念学习模型具有对应关系；扩散模型可被视为一种连续、可插值且可扩展的概念层级模型，并在中等噪声水平出现类似人类认知的“基本层级”。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类会将知识组织为具有嵌套抽象层次的概念分类体系，并在“基本层级”上以最低认知成本识别和命名对象。经典Cobweb模型能够通过增量学习和类别效用最大化解释这一现象，但其离散树结构在规模和连续概念表示方面存在局限。本文旨在探讨扩散模型的概率结构是否隐式实现了类似的概念形成与分类计算，从而连接生成模型与认知科学中的概念层级理论。

Method: 作者从理论上比较Cobweb与扩散模型的结构：将扩散模型在不同噪声水平下的数据边缘分布视为原始数据分布的高斯平滑，其模态随噪声变化形成概念层级；进一步论证两者在层级密度建模、带高斯原型的层级贝叶斯表示、通过沿得分函数方向降低不确定性的分类机制，以及基本层级的涌现方面具有对应关系。随后，在MNIST和Fashion-MNIST上通过模态寻找恢复扩散模型的层级，并将其基本层级与Cobweb模型进行比较。

Result: 理论分析表明，扩散模型的中间噪声层级可对应于基本层级；该位置与已有关于反向扩散过程在中等噪声阶段确定样本类别身份的分析相一致。实验部分通过MNIST和Fashion-MNIST恢复扩散层级，并比较两种模型的基本层级，以检验二者的对应性。摘要未报告具体数值指标或显著性结果，但总体声称实验支持这种结构上的对应关系。

Conclusion: 扩散模型可以被重新理解为一种概念形成的认知模型：它不像Cobweb那样学习离散的增量式树，而是在单一得分场中编码连续、可插值的概念层级。因此，扩散模型为Cobweb提供了更具可扩展性和连续性的实现，同时也为理解扩散生成过程中的类别抽象与概念组织提供了认知解释。

Abstract: Humans organize knowledge into a taxonomy of concepts with nested levels of abstraction and a \emph{basic level} at which people recognize and name objects with the least cognitive effort. Cobweb is a classic cognitive account of this ability, an incremental learner that builds a probabilistic concept hierarchy by maximizing category utility. We argue that diffusion models, although designed for image synthesis, implicitly perform the same computation. The noisy marginals of a diffusion model are Gaussian smoothings of the data distribution, and the modes of these marginals form a hierarchy that corresponds to a Cobweb tree of probabilistic prototypes in four respects. Both are hierarchical density models, both are hierarchical-Bayesian models with Gaussian prototypes, both treat categorization as score-following that reduces uncertainty, and in both a basic level emerges. We locate this basic level for a diffusion model at an intermediate noise level, where recent analyses show that the reverse process commits to the class identity of a sample. The two models differ mainly in how they represent and learn the taxonomy. Cobweb learns a discrete tree incrementally, whereas a diffusion model encodes a continuous, interpolable hierarchy in a single learned score field fit to the data distribution. We test the correspondence on MNIST and Fashion-MNIST by recovering the diffusion hierarchy through mode-finding and comparing the basic levels of the two models. This reframes diffusion as a cognitive model of concept formation and offers Cobweb a continuous, scalable instantiation.

</details>


### [182] [Anchoring Clinical Events in Time: UID-Preserving Multimodal Reconstruction and Source-Grounded Adjudication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13062)
*Sayantan Kumar,Nicolas Grimaldi,Jack Cummins,Jeremy C. Weiss*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个保留事件唯一标识（UID）的临床时间线重建与评估框架，将叙事事件、原文片段、结构化证据和时间戳来源行关联起来，并引入GAVEL大语言模型评审器比较候选时间线。实验显示，GLM 5.2多模态修订相较文本-only版本提高了时间一致性且不牺牲事件召回；UID主要提升事件保留，来源行链接主要帮助时间定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床时间线对于治疗窗口分析和避免时间泄漏建模至关重要，但出院总结常打乱或模糊事件顺序，结构化EHR表格也只能覆盖部分病程。现有方法缺少事件发生级别的身份保持与可追溯来源，导致时间重建和评估不够可靠，因此需要一个能够连接叙事事件、结构化证据及其原始来源的统一框架，并需要更细粒度的自动评估方法。

Method: 提出UID-preserving框架：为每个叙事事件发生实例分配并保持唯一标识，将其绑定到文本来源片段；通过文本-only估计、结构化证据检索、带时间戳的来源行对齐以及联合修订来重建时间线。进一步提出GAVEL这一LLM评审器，对两个UID对齐的候选时间线与叙事文本、结构化病历进行比较。实验覆盖6个开放权重模型和40份混合重症监护出院总结，并进行消融实验、盲法人工复核及受控裁决。

Result: GLM 5.2的多模态修订相较文本-only版本提高了时间一致性，同时没有降低事件恢复率，并且整体表现具有与临床医生标注竞争的水平；其他模型的修订收益较小且总体性能较低。消融结果表明，UID主要负责保持事件不被遗漏，来源行链接主要支持事件的时间定位。盲法人工复核支持GAVEL的大多数判断；受控裁决支持GLM 5.2多模态版本优于文本-only版本，但未支持DeepSeek V3.2存在同样优势。作者还报告事件恢复率提高43%。

Conclusion: UID与来源行级溯源相结合，可以同时提升临床事件保留、时间定位和结果可解释性；GAVEL可作为时间线匹配与时间评估的补充工具。该框架的主要贡献是实现了事件发生级别的来源追踪，并为临床时间线重建和评估提供了可审计方案。不过，实验样本量较小，模型间收益不一致，且43%的提升指标在摘要中缺乏明确基线和统计显著性说明，因此其泛化性仍需更大规模、多机构和更严格的临床验证。

Abstract: Clinical timelines support treatment-window analysis and leakage-free modeling, but discharge summaries often obscure chronology and structured EHR tables describe only part of the patient course. We present a UID-preserving framework that links each narrative event occurrence to its source span and retains that identity through text-only estimation, structured-evidence retrieval, timestamped source-row grounding, and joint revision. We also present GAVEL, an LLM judge that compares two UID-aligned timelines against the narrative and structured record, to augment prior matching and temporal assessments. Across six open-weight models and 40 mixed-critical-care summaries, the GLM 5.2 multimodal revision, as compared to its text-only variant, improved temporal agreement without reducing event recovery and performed competitively with clinician annotations, while other model revisions showed smaller gains and lower overall performance. Ablations showed that UIDs primarily preserve event retention, whereas source-row linkage supports temporal placement. Blinded human review upheld most GAVEL findings, and controlled adjudication favored multimodal over text-only GLM 5.2 but did not for DeepSeek V3.2. In developing the UID and judge pipeline, we are able to demonstrate 43\% increased event recovery, a framework competitive with clinician annotations, and a system with occurrence-level provenance for both reconstruction and evaluation.

</details>


### [183] [Autonomous Research for Open-Ended Problems: A Case Study on Telecom Ticket Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13073)
*Junghyun Min,Huseyin Uzunalioglu,Mohamed Trabelsi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文研究将自主机器学习研究框架应用于开放性、工业级的电信工单检索任务。结果表明，自主研究在超参数优化等狭窄搜索空间中表现良好，但在表示设计、模型架构和训练数据生成等需要直觉与创造力的环节仍不如人类。人机协作是更优方案：仅需少量人工监督，自主系统可在约10周内以较低成本达到人工10个月工作所获最佳性能的约90%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有端到端自主机器学习研究框架通常只在搜索空间狭窄的问题上取得成功，例如语言建模和生物医学基准任务。论文旨在探讨这类自主研究能否迁移到开放性、工业级机器学习问题，并评估其能力边界、效率、成本以及与人类研究者协作的必要性。

Method: 论文以电信工单检索为案例任务。该任务在表示方式、模型架构和训练数据生成方面具有较高自由度，适合检验开放性自主研究。研究者使用商业和开源智能体执行机器学习研究流程，并比较自主研究与人工研究在性能、耗时和成本方面的表现，同时分析自主系统在不同搜索空间中的能力及其运行开销。

Result: 自主研究在狭窄的超参数优化空间中表现突出，但在需要人类式直觉、创造力和整体方案设计的开放性环节存在局限。仅有少量人工监督时，系统在10周内达到Recall@1为0.34，相当于人工10个月取得的0.38的约90%；单次Cursor运行成本最高约200美元。研究还发现，自主研究伴随一定的运维和操作开销。

Conclusion: 自主机器学习研究有望显著缩短工业级模型开发周期并降低成本，但目前尚不能完全替代人类研究者。对于开放性机器学习问题，最有效的模式是人机协作：由自主系统承担实验执行、局部搜索和超参数优化，由人类负责问题建模、关键设计决策、创新方案提出及结果判断。

Abstract: Recent breakthroughs in LLM-based systems and their abilities in problem solving and coding have allowed progress in the AI for Science paradigm, potentially replacing human roles in machine learning (ML) research. However, while several frameworks of fully autonomous end-to-end ML research have been proposed, successful implementations of them are often limited to problems with narrow search spaces, like language modeling or biomedical ML benchmarks. In this paper, we explore how autonomous research can be adapted to solve open-ended, industry-grade ML problems, by considering a case study: telecom ticket retrieval, an open-ended task with degrees of freedom in representation, architecture, and training data generation. We discover that autonomous research for open-ended problems with commercial and open-source agents shows both promise and limitations: while autonomous research can excel in narrow hyperparameter optimization, it lacks human-like intuition and creativity and requires operational overhead. Even with minimal human supervision, autonomous research can reach $90\%$ of state-of-the-art performance (0.34 vs. 0.38 Recall@1) in a much shorter time period (10 weeks vs. 10 months of human work) at a modest cost (up to \$200 per Cursor campaign). Our empirical evidence recommends that human researchers and autonomous research frameworks work together for best results in ML research.

</details>


### [184] [Embodied-BenchForge: A Closed-Loop Agentic Workflow for Embodied Benchmark Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13082)
*Baoyang Jiang,Fengchun Zhang,Leyuan Wang,Haotian Li,Yida Wang,Zhe Ji,Jinshan Lai,Xi Ren,Danyang Li,Zheng Yang,Jianwei Hu,Qiang Ma*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Embodied-BenchForge 是一个面向具身智能基准构建的闭环智能体框架，将用户评价意图转化为完整、可验证、可修复的基准产物。它通过技能编排、产物依赖图、需求驱动验证与溯源修复，降低多步构建中的缺陷传播，并生成覆盖离线问答和交互执行的多个基准。实验表明，所构建基准能够区分多模态大语言模型与具身智能体的能力，且验证修复机制有效、局部恢复高效、技能可跨基准复用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有具身基准构建方法通常只覆盖单一阶段，或依赖预定义环境和任务类型，难以根据用户指定的评价意图自动生成完整基准。更关键的是，多步骤构建会产生相互依赖的中间产物，而现有流程往往缺少针对产物的独立验证，导致局部错误未经检测地向下游传播，最终损害基准质量。因此，论文旨在构建一个通用、自动化且具备闭环验证和修复能力的具身基准生成框架。

Method: 论文提出 Embodied-BenchForge，并将基准构建形式化为“闭环基准综合”（Closed-Loop Benchmark Synthesis）：一方面进行前向产物生成，另一方面执行后向验证与修复。其核心包括：1）技能编排产物综合，将带类型、可复用的技能组合为可执行工作流；2）产物依赖图，显式记录中间产物及其依赖关系；3）需求引导的验证与修复，为不同产物定义专属契约，并利用溯源信息定位故障，在验证失败时执行局部重运行或上游回滚。框架最终用于构建离线问答赛道中的六个基准，以及交互式具身赛道中包含220个可执行任务的一个基准。

Result: 框架构建出的基准覆盖多种具身场景。对代表性多模态大语言模型和具身智能体的评测显示，这些基准能够区分模型在基于观察的理解能力和闭环执行能力方面的差异。质量评估与消融实验验证了基准本身的质量，以及验证—修复机制的有效性。进一步的修复分析表明，框架能够进行高效的局部恢复；技能复用分析则表明，同一套技能可以跨不同基准复用。

Conclusion: Embodied-BenchForge 证明了将具身基准构建设计为带有依赖追踪、产物级契约验证和溯源修复的闭环流程是可行且有效的。该方法提升了基准构建的可靠性、可扩展性和效率，并支持跨场景、跨基准复用构建能力。不过，摘要未进一步说明其对更大规模环境、更多任务类型或真实机器人平台的泛化能力，这些仍是后续研究方向。

Abstract: Agentic systems offer a promising way to automate embodied benchmark construction, but existing approaches typically cover isolated stages or remain specialized to predefined environments and task families. More importantly, multi-step construction produces dependent intermediate artifacts that are often passed downstream without artifact-specific verification, allowing local defects to propagate into the final benchmark. We present Embodied-BenchForge, an agentic framework that transforms user-specified evaluation intents into complete embodied benchmark artifacts. It formulates construction as Closed-Loop Benchmark Synthesis, integrating forward artifact synthesis with backward verification and repair. Skill-Orchestrated Artifact Synthesis composes typed and reusable skills into executable workflows, while an artifact dependency graph records intermediate outputs and their dependencies. Requirement-Guided Verification and Repair applies artifact-specific contracts throughout construction and uses provenance to trigger local re-execution or upstream rollback when verification fails. Embodied-BenchForge constructs six benchmarks covering diverse embodied scenarios in the Offline EQA Track, together with one interactive benchmark containing 220 executable tasks in the Interactive Embodied Track. Evaluations of representative MLLMs and embodied agents show that the benchmarks distinguish model capabilities in both observation-based understanding and closed-loop execution. Quality assessment and ablations validate benchmark quality and the effectiveness of verification and repair, while repair and skill-reuse analyses demonstrate efficient localized recovery and cross-benchmark reusability.

</details>


### [185] [CMA-OT: Hierarchical Expert Supervision for Dance-to-Music Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13118)
*Jinting Wang,Chenxing Li,Dong Yu,Li Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出CMA-OT，用课程学习和尺度感知最优传输，将外部音乐专家的多尺度层级知识逐步对齐到舞蹈到音乐生成器的潜在特征中，从而缓解舞蹈稀疏语义与音乐密集结构之间的语义鸿沟。实验表明，该方法在节奏同步、感知质量和整体音乐生成性能上达到先进水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 舞蹈到音乐生成需要根据舞蹈视频合成在节奏和风格上匹配的音乐，但舞蹈通常只提供稀疏的节奏、动作和风格线索，而音乐创作还涉及曲式结构、配器和表现力等密集信息。现有方法主要依赖这些稀疏线索，并仅对最终音频进行监督，导致生成器难以学习高质量的音乐表示，生成结果在音乐性和结构连贯性方面受限。因此，论文旨在引入更丰富、更具层次性的音乐知识监督，以弥合跨模态语义差距。

Method: 提出课程引导的多尺度表示对齐方法CMA-OT。首先，利用外部音乐专家提供层级化、多尺度的音乐表示监督生成器潜在特征。其次，设计课程引导的多尺度学习策略，按照由易到难、由粗到细的过程，逐步将音乐专家知识迁移给生成器，以提升训练稳定性和表示学习效果。最后，提出尺度感知最优传输对齐机制，针对不同专家尺度之间的语义和结构差异，以及舞蹈与音乐在时间上的错位，建立层级专家表示与生成器潜在特征之间的软对应关系，实现细粒度时序对齐。

Result: 在两个数据集上的大量实验显示，CMA-OT在节奏同步、感知质量和整体音乐生成性能方面均取得当前先进结果，说明层级音乐监督、课程式知识迁移和尺度感知对齐能够有效提升D2M生成质量。摘要未提供具体数值、基线名称或消融实验结果。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，仅监督最终音频不足以解决舞蹈到音乐生成中的跨模态语义鸿沟；将外部音乐专家的多尺度知识直接用于生成器潜在表示学习是有效方向。课程学习保证了层级知识迁移的稳定性，尺度感知最优传输则增强了不同时间尺度和结构层次下的细粒度对齐，最终提升了音乐的同步性、质量与结构表现。

Abstract: Dance-to-music (D2M) generation aims to synthesize music that is rhythmically and stylistically aligned with dance videos. A key challenge arises from the semantic mismatch between sparse dance cues, such as rhythm and style, and the dense information required for music composition, including structure, instrumentation, and expressive dynamics. Existing methods typically rely on these sparse cues and supervise only the final audio output, resulting in poorly learned music representations and generated music with limited musicality and structural coherence. To address these issues, we propose Curriculum-guided Multi-scale representation Alignment with scale-aware Optimal Transport (CMA-OT), a novel paradigm that leverages an external music expert to provide hierarchical supervision for the generator's latent features, bridging the semantic gap and enhancing representation learning. To effectively incorporate hierarchical supervision, we introduce a curriculum-guided multi-scale learning strategy that progressively transfers musical knowledge from the expert to the music generator, enabling stable and effective representation learning. Moreover, to accommodate the semantic and structural variations across different expert scales and achieve fine-grained alignment under temporal mismatch, we propose a scale-aware optimal transport alignment mechanism, which models soft correspondences between hierarchical expert representations and the generator's latent features. Extensive experiments on two datasets demonstrate that CMA-OT achieves state-of-the-art performance in rhythmic synchronization, perceptual quality, and overall music generation.

</details>


### [186] [A Hybrid LSTM-XGBoost Framework for Multi-Horizon Stock Return Prediction Across Diversified Equity Portfolios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13125)
*Seif ElDein Mostafa,Yahia Ahmed,Farah Datwish,Marwa Solayman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种LSTM与XGBoost串联的混合架构，用于基于多只美国股票数据进行30、90、252和365个交易日的多时间跨度收益率预测，并构建投资评分框架支持股票排序与决策。模型在短期RMSE和多数股票表现上优于或匹配基线，但长期方向准确率受到样本中正收益高基准率的显著影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 股票收益预测面临非平稳性、非线性和低信噪比等问题，单一深度学习模型或传统机器学习模型难以同时有效捕捉时序动态与人工技术特征。因此，研究动机是融合LSTM的序列表示学习能力和XGBoost对结构化特征的非线性拟合能力，以提升跨股票、跨预测期限的收益预测效果，并为投资组合排序提供辅助依据。

Method: 研究采用两阶段混合架构：首先，使用包含两层、每层64个隐藏单元的LSTM处理60个交易日窗口内的5项连续市场特征，生成64维时序嵌入；随后将该嵌入与14项人工构造的技术指标拼接，形成78维特征向量，并输入XGBoost回归器。模型在14只、覆盖6个行业的美国股票组成的 pooled 数据集上训练，采用严格按时间顺序的数据划分，并进行逐股票MinMaxScaling以避免前视偏差。XGBoost通过3折交叉验证网格搜索调参，分别预测30、90、252和365个交易日的收益。研究还构建了多时间跨度预测驱动的综合投资评分框架，并以LSTM单模型、仅XGBoost模型和始终预测正收益的朴素方向基线进行比较。

Result: 混合模型在30日预测期限上取得0.0949的测试RMSE，约为独立LSTM基线的三分之一；在多数股票上，其表现略优于或至少匹配仅XGBoost基线。方向准确率随预测期限增加，在365日达到97.6%。但作者指出，长期方向准确率主要反映样本中长期正收益的高基准率，因此以始终预测正收益作为朴素基线，并认为短期预测中超过该基线的增益更具信息价值。

Conclusion: LSTM提取的时序嵌入与XGBoost对技术指标的非线性建模具有互补性，所提出的混合模型在短期收益预测方面显示出明显优势，并可扩展至多期限预测和股票排序。然而，长期方向准确率不能单独视为模型能力的充分证据，必须相对于正收益基准进行评估。总体而言，该框架更适合作为投资组合筛选和决策支持工具，而非直接证明其能够稳定获得超额收益。

Abstract: Accurate prediction of equity returns remains a major challenge in computational finance due to the non-stationary, nonlinear, and low signal-to-noise ratio nature of financial time series. This paper proposes a hybrid two-stage architecture that combines a long short-term memory (LSTM) network with an XGBoost gradient-boosted regressor for multi-horizon stock return prediction across a diversified panel of 14 U.S. equities spanning six industry sectors. The LSTM component, comprising two stacked layers with 64 hidden units, processes 60-day sliding windows of five sequential market features to produce 64-dimensional temporal embeddings that encode learned sequential market dynamics. These embeddings are concatenated with 14 hand-crafted technical indicators to form a 78-dimensional hybrid feature vector, which is subsequently passed to an XGBoost regressor tuned via 3-fold cross-validation grid search. The framework is trained on a multi-stock pooled corpus using strict chronological splits and per-stock MinMaxScaling to prevent look-ahead bias, and evaluated across four prediction horizons of 30, 90, 252, and 365 trading days. Experimental results demonstrate that the hybrid model achieves a test RMSE of 0.0949 on the 30-day horizon, roughly one-third that of the standalone LSTM baseline, while marginally matching or surpassing the XGBoost-Only baseline across the majority of stocks. Directional accuracy rises with horizon length, reaching 97.6% at 365 days; we show, however, that this largely tracks the high base rate of positive long-horizon returns in the sample, and we therefore benchmark directional accuracy against a naive always-positive predictor and treat the above-base-rate gap at short horizons as the more informative signal. A composite investment scoring framework derived from multi-horizon predictions is further proposed to support portfolio ranking and decision support.

</details>


### [187] [Rethinking Heterogeneous System Disaggregation for Subquadratic Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13134)
*Arya Tschand,Yaosheng Fu,Vikram Sharma Mailthody,Nicolai Oswald,Po-An Tsai,Ritchie Zhao,Oreste Villa,Vijay Janapa Reddi,Karu Sankaralingam*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 SQD（SubQuadratic Disaggregation），针对采用稀疏、线性或滑动窗口注意力的前沿语言模型，将解码过程按二次注意力与次二次注意力的计算强度和内存占用进行细粒度异构拆分，而非沿用传统的算子类型拆分。在异构 DRAM/SRAM 系统上，SQD 显著提升能效、吞吐率和可实现延迟：相较强 GPU-only 基线，GLM 5.2、Nemotron 3 Ultra 和 Gemma 4 31B 的平均 tokens/J 分别提升 53%、31% 和 56%；相较注意力-FFN 拆分基线，延迟收紧 1.2–1.5 倍，吞吐最高提升 3.6 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理服务系统主要围绕稠密注意力设计解耦策略，通常按算子类型拆分计算。然而，采用次二次注意力的语言模型在算术强度、KV 缓存访问模式和静态内存占用方面具有不同特征，使传统拆分方式无法充分利用 DRAM-based 与 SRAM-only 异构硬件。因此，论文旨在设计一种面向次二次注意力模型的细粒度解耦方案，以降低推理内存和计算开销，并提升吞吐率与能效。

Method: 提出 SQD（SubQuadratic Disaggregation）框架，核心是按注意力复杂度和内存行为拆分解码，而不是简单按算子类型拆分。对于稀疏注意力模型，将解码划分为需要遍历完整 KV 缓存的 top-k selection，以及具有静态内存占用的 top-k attention 加 FFN；对于线性注意力和滑动窗口注意力模型，则将解码划分为稠密注意力层，以及次二次注意力层加 FFN。论文在调整后的 8×B200 异构系统代理上进行实验，并使用 Rubin 加 LPX 系统的分析模型，在固定功耗预算下评估延迟和吞吐率，同时分析芯片与互连资源配置。

Result: 在 8×B200 异构系统代理上，相较最强 GPU-only 基线，平均 tokens/J 在 GLM 5.2、Nemotron 3 Ultra 和 Gemma 4 31B 上分别提升 53%、31% 和 56%。在 Rubin+LPX 分析模型中，相较最佳的注意力-FFN 解耦基线，SQD 可实现 1.2–1.5 倍更严格的可达延迟约束，以及最高 3.6 倍的吞吐率提升。实验还揭示了面向次二次注意力推理的芯片容量与互连带宽配置规律。

Conclusion: 将推理解码按二次与次二次注意力的计算和内存特征进行解耦，比传统的算子级解耦更适合新一代次二次注意力语言模型。SQD 能够有效利用 DRAM 与 SRAM 异构系统，在能效、延迟和吞吐率方面取得显著收益，并为未来异构推理系统的芯片及互连设计提供架构依据。

Abstract: Frontier language models are more aggressively using subquadratic attention to reduce the memory footprint and compute requirements during inference while still delivering frontier accuracy. While existing systems make dense attention-centric disaggregated serving decisions, we show that disaggregating inference around the unique arithmetic intensity and memory footprint of subquadratic attention LLMs can achieve significant throughput and energy efficiency gains on emerging DRAM-based and SRAM-only heterogeneous systems.
  We introduce SQD (SubQuadratic Disaggregation), a fine-grained heterogeneous disaggregation scheme that splits decode by quadratic and subquadratic attention rather than by operator type, and that applies across subquadratic attention variants. For sparse attention LLMs, we disaggregate decode into top-k selection, which must index through the full KV, and top-k attention plus FFN, which have static memory footprints. For linear and sliding-window attention LLMs, we disaggregate decode into dense attention layers and subquadratic attention layers plus FFN. In an adjusted 8xB200 heterogeneous system proxy, we observe average tokens/J improvements of 53% on GLM 5.2, 31% on Nemotron 3 Ultra, and 56% on Gemma 4 31B over the strongest GPU-only baselines. In an analytical model of a Rubin plus LPX system with fixed power budgets, we observe 1.2x to 1.5x tighter achievable latencies and up to 3.6x higher throughput over the best baseline of attention-FFN disaggregation. Our experiments also reveal architectural insights on chip and interconnect provisioning for next-generation heterogeneous systems serving subquadratic attention.

</details>


<div id='cs.IT'></div>

# cs.IT [[Back]](#toc)

### [188] [Feedback Capacity of Stationary Gaussian Channels: An Optimal Schalkwijk-Kailath Scheme](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12069)
*David Fay,Oron Sabag*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文证明：对于具有有理功率谱密度的平稳高斯加性噪声反馈信道，反馈容量优化中的最优输入不会包含反馈无关的高斯分量；基于这一更强结论，可从任意最优解构造显式的Schalkwijk–Kailath（SK）编码方案，以低于反馈容量的任意速率通信，并使最大错误概率呈双指数衰减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kim的反馈容量变分刻画及其关于“最优输入不含反馈无关高斯分量”的断言，支撑了SK型简单编码方案达到反馈容量的结论。然而，已有证明存在缺口，导致SK方案及依赖该断言的后续结果的最优性尚未完全确立。本文旨在修补这一理论缺口，并进一步建立更强的最优性结论。

Method: 将反馈容量问题表述为凸优化问题，并对其进行扰动分析。针对噪声具有有理功率谱密度的平稳高斯信道，证明每个优化解都会将零功率分配给反馈无关分量。在此基础上，从任意容量优化器显式构造SK型编码方案。

Result: 证明了反馈无关高斯分量移除断言对该类信道成立，而且结论对所有优化器成立，而非仅存在某个满足该性质的优化器。进一步证明，所构造的SK编码方案可实现所有低于反馈容量的通信速率，并具有双指数衰减的最大错误概率。

Conclusion: 本文完整确立了有理功率谱密度平稳高斯噪声反馈信道中SK型方案的容量最优性及其可靠性优势，修复了先前证明中的缺口，并为相关后续结果提供了严格理论基础。

Abstract: We consider channels with additive colored Gaussian noise and noiseless feedback. Kim's seminal work derived a stationary variational characterization of feedback capacity and further asserted that the capacity-achieving stationary input need not contain a feedback-independent Gaussian component. These results led to the construction of a simple coding scheme, based on the Schalkwijk--Kailath (SK) refinement principle, which was shown to be capacity-achieving. A recent note identified a gap in the proof of the feedback-independent component-removal assertion, thereby leaving the optimality of the SK scheme and subsequent results that rely on it incomplete. In this paper, we prove the component-removal assertion for channels with stationary Gaussian noise that has a rational power spectral density. Our proof uses a perturbation analysis of a convex optimization formulation of feedback capacity and, indeed, shows that every optimizer assigns zero power to the feedback-independent component. Using this stronger property, we construct from any optimizer an explicit SK coding scheme that achieves every rate below feedback capacity with doubly-exponentially decaying maximal error probability.

</details>


### [189] [Receiver-Surface Hit Patterns via Legendre Approximation for Molecular Signal Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12089)
*Yasin Bastug,Erencem Ozbey,H. Birkan Yilmaz*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 该论文研究分子通信中的发射状态检测问题，利用球形接收器表面分子到达位置所携带的方向信息，构建基于勒让德多项式的信号检测器及序列检测器，以优于仅计数的检测方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统分子通信检测通常只利用接收分子数量和到达时间，忽略了分子在球形接收器表面的吸收位置。表面位置包含与发射器—接收器几何关系相关的方向特征，能够提供额外判别信息。因此，论文旨在挖掘这种方向性信息，提升发射器是否处于通信状态的检测性能，同时保持检测方法的计算效率。

Method: 首先对球形接收器表面的分子命中密度进行建模，并采用勒让德多项式展开，以紧凑地表示分子到达位置的方向分布。在此基础上，设计基于勒让德展开系数的分子信号检测器；进一步将该思想扩展到基于勒让德特征的维特比序列检测器，以处理具有符号间干扰或时间序列依赖的连续检测任务。方法重点利用接收表面的空间命中分布，而非仅使用分子总数。

Result: 摘要表明，所提出的单符号检测器和序列检测器均能利用分子到达位置的方向特征，相较于仅计数检测实现更好的检测性能。同时，这些方法具有几何感知能力和较高效率。不过，摘要未给出具体的误检率、检测准确率、信噪比增益或复杂度数值。

Conclusion: 论文证明了球形接收器表面分子吸收位置中的方向信息具有实际检测价值。基于勒让德多项式的检测框架能够在较低复杂度下融合空间分布信息，为分子通信中的几何感知检测提供了有效方案，并可进一步用于序列级信号检测。

Abstract: Detecting whether a transmitter is actively communicating with a receiver is a fundamental problem in molecular communications. A spherical receiver may measure not only the number and arrival times of absorbed molecules, but also their absorption locations on the receiver surface. These locations contain a directional signature that is lost in count-only detection. In this letter, we develop a molecular signal detector based on a Legendre polynomial expansion of the receiver-surface hit density and extend it to a Legendre-based Viterbi sequence detector. By exploiting the surface-level directional signature of molecular arrivals, the proposed detectors provide geometry-aware, efficient, and better-performing alternatives to count-only detection.

</details>


### [190] [Joint Random Access and Localization in Cell-Free User-Centric Networks with Frequency-Selective Fading Channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12140)
*Simon Tarboush,Eleni Gkiouzepi,Giuseppe Caire*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究面向无小区用户中心网络的随机接入方案，联合解决随机接入前导检测与用户定位问题。针对帧同步但码片异步、LoS/NLoS共存及频率选择性多径等现实信道特征，提出统一建模框架，并比较基于Zadoff-Chu序列的传统方案与扩展到频率选择性衰落的多源AMP方案。结果表明，AMP方案前导检测性能更优，而两种方案的定位性能均优秀且相近。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无小区用户中心网络中存在大量地理分散、具有突发和间歇业务的用户。系统需要在随机接入时隙中检测未知接入用户发送的前导，并快速定位用户以分配合适的无线单元集群。现有研究通常忽略码片异步、部分用户-无线单元链路中的视距传播，以及非视距多径导致的频率选择性衰落，因此难以准确反映实际网络。本文旨在建立更完整的空间一致性网络级信道模型，并提升联合前导检测与定位能力。

Method: 采用位置相关的分区随机接入码本，使不同地理区域的用户使用对应的随机接入前导子集。在统一框架下进行联合检测与定位，并考虑帧同步但码片异步传输、LoS与NLoS传播及频率选择性多径。比较两类方案：一是基于Zadoff-Chu序列、扩展3GPP两步RACH的传统方案；二是将多源近似消息传递（AMP）扩展至多径频率选择性衰落的方案。针对两者分别设计近似广义似然比检验（GLRT）前导检测器和最大似然位置估计器，并通过超分辨率方法细化定位结果，利用LoS分量中隐含的接收信号强度、到达角和到达时间差等信息。

Result: 数值结果显示，基于AMP的方案在前导检测方面明显优于传统ZC方案。两种方案在用户定位方面均表现出优秀性能，且总体定位能力相近。

Conclusion: 在包含现实传播与同步误差因素的无小区用户中心网络中，AMP方法更适合提升随机接入前导检测性能；而借助LoS信息及联合最大似然定位，两类方案都能够实现高质量用户定位。因此，检测性能是两种方案的主要差异，而定位性能并非主要瓶颈。

Abstract: We study random access (RACH) schemes for cell-free (CF) user-centric networks to handle many geographically distributed users with sporadic traffic and intermittent activity. The RACH must allow the system to: 1) detect preambles sent by the (yet unknown) random access users in the RACH slot; 2) localize them for fast allocation of user-centric radio-unit (RU) clusters. Most prior work uses simplified models, neglecting frame-synchronous but chip-asynchronous transmission, possible line-of-sight (LoS) propagation for certain user-RU pairs, and multipath non-line-of-sight (NLoS) propagation yielding frequency-selective channels. Building on our previous work, we consider location-dependent partitioned random access codebooks where users in a geographic area (location) use the corresponding subset of random access preambles. We present a unified framework for joint detection and localization over a spatially consistent network-wide channel model, incorporating these neglected aspects. We evaluate two schemes: 1) a ``legacy'' scheme using Zadoff-Chu (ZC) sequences, extending the 3GPP 2-step RACH to the CF case; 2) our multisource approximate message passing (AMP) approach extended to multipath frequency-selective fading. For both schemes, we develop novel approximated GLRT preamble detection and Maximum-Likelihood position estimators with super-resolution refinement, implicitly exploiting received signal strength, angle of arrival, and time-difference of arrival information embedded into LoS components. Numerical results show that the AMP-based scheme achieves superior preamble detection, while both schemes have similar and excellent localization capability.

</details>


### [191] [Random Access and Localization in Cell-Free User-Centric Networks with Multipath Channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12141)
*Simon Tarboush,Eleni Gkiouzepi,Giuseppe Caire*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 论文研究面向无小区用户中心网络的基于位置相关随机接入码本的RACH方案，比较基于Zadoff–Chu序列与GLRT的频域/时延未知检测方法和基于多源AMP的方法，并利用LoS分量中的AoA/TDoA信息实现随机接入用户定位。结果表明，AMP在用户检测方面通常更优，而两种方案的定位性能接近，频域方案略占优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统随机接入机制需要支持新用户接入、资源请求以及潜在的用户定位。在无小区用户中心网络中，用户可能分布于不同地理区域，位置相关的随机接入码本有助于降低接入冲突并利用空间传播特征。此前工作尚未充分覆盖具有多径和一定范围内视距传播的实际信道，也未系统比较不同检测框架及其定位能力，因此本文进行扩展研究。

Method: 研究设置位置相关随机接入码本，使特定地理区域的用户使用对应随机接入前导码。针对多径且包含一定半径内LoS传播的信道，提出并比较两类方案：第一类采用Zadoff–Chu序列和广义似然比检验（GLRT），以处理未知传播时延，思想上对应扩展到无小区网络的3GPP两步RACH；第二类采用多源近似消息传递（AMP）。两类方案均进一步采用近似最大似然定位方法，直接根据检测到的RACH前导码估计用户位置，并隐式利用LoS分量中包含的到达角（AoA）和到达时间差（TDoA）信息。

Result: 仿真结果显示，AMP方案在随机接入用户检测方面总体表现更好。两种方案的用户定位能力总体相近，但基于频域处理的方案具有轻微优势。

Conclusion: 在所研究的无小区用户中心网络和信道条件下，AMP更适合提升随机接入用户检测性能；然而，若重点关注基于RACH信号的定位，ZC序列结合GLRT的频域方案在定位方面略有优势。研究表明，位置相关码本与LoS传播特征可以同时支持随机接入检测和用户定位。

Abstract: In a wireless network, the initial/random access mechanism (RACH) allows idle/new users to join the network and (possibly) request allocated transmission resources for subsequent traffic. Building on our own previous work, for cell-free user-centric networks, we consider location-dependent random access codebooks such that users in a certain geographic area (location) make use of the corresponding set of random access preambles (codewords). We expand our previous work in two ways: (1) we consider multipath channels with line-of-sight (LoS) propagation within a given radius; (2) we consider two different approaches. The first makes use of Zadoff-Chu (ZC) sequences and GLRT detection to cope with the unknown delay, and it is conceptually similar to the 3GPP 2-step RACH specification (here extended to the cell-free case). The second builds on our previous work on multisource approximate message passing (AMP). For both schemes, we also consider a novel near Maximum-Likelihood approach for localization of the random access users directly from the detected RACH preambles, implicitly using angle of arrival and time difference of arrival information embedded into the LoS components. Simulation results show that the AMP approach achieves generally better performance for random access user detection, while both approaches have similar localization capability with a slight superiority for the frequency-domain scheme.

</details>


### [192] [Error-Rate Reduction in LDPC Decoding via Bit-Aligned Temporal Reinforcement in Parallel Probabilistic-Bit Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12389)
*Naoya Onizawa,Takahiro Hanyu*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究带时间响应记忆的概率比特退火方法用于随机正则(3,6) LDPC码译码，提出加性响应路径规则，在随机读出前保存并复用每个比特的饱和响应；相较无记忆pSA，在块长96、192、288上平均比特错误率分别降低33.5%、74.8%和81.8%，并通过轨迹分析说明收益来自更快进入正确码字吸引域及更强的获得后稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 概率比特(p-bit)适合随机推断和并行优化，但高度并行的Jacobi更新可能改变群体动力学，导致LDPC译码性能受限。已有方法中的时间状态并非都能带来有效增益，因此需要明确：应保留何种历史信息，以及如何将其重新注入随机动力学，才能改善译码的搜索与稳定性。

Method: 针对随机正则(3,6) LDPC码，采用Jacobi型p-bit退火并结合随机部分激活。核心方法是加性响应路径规则：保存每个比特的饱和响应，并在随机读出前复用该响应。通过高统计量仿真，对代表性块长96、192、288的码进行独立参数优化，并与无记忆概率模拟退火(pSA)比较。另行测试静态增益、归一化平均、响应打乱，以及仅调节系数的同比特二元状态反馈；同时考察随机初始化和信道硬判决初始化，并在30个独立码实例上使用固定的参数—读出方案评估跨码泛化能力。

Result: 相对无记忆pSA，所提方法在块长96、192、288上的汇总比特错误率分别降低33.5%、74.8%和81.8%。静态增益、归一化平均、响应打乱及同比特二元反馈均无法复现完整的饱和响应收益。轨迹分析表明，性能提升主要源于更容易获得与信道一致的有效码字吸引域，以及进入该吸引域后更好的稳定性；从随机起点和信道硬判决起点出发时，获得优势仍然存在。在全部30个独立码实现上，固定的加性参数—读出方案同时取得比特错误率和帧错误率优势，且无需针对单个码重新调参。

Conclusion: 时间状态对概率退火计算的影响取决于所保留的具体量及其重新注入随机动力学的方式。饱和响应路径记忆不仅提供一般性的历史平滑，而是改变了译码轨迹的吸引域获取和稳定机制，因此能显著提升LDPC译码性能，并表现出跨码实现的稳健性。

Abstract: Probabilistic bits (p-bits) provide a physical and algorithmic primitive for stochastic inference, but highly parallel updates can alter their collective dynamics. We study the decoding of random-regular (3, 6) low-density parity-check (LDPC) codes using Jacobi-type p-bit annealing with stochastic partial activation. An additive response-path rule stores each bit's saturated response and reuses it before stochastic readout. High-statistics simulations with independent parameter optimization for each method show pooled bit-error-rate reductions of 33.5%, 74.8%, and 81.8% relative to memoryless probabilistic simulated annealing (pSA) for representative codes of block lengths 96, 192, and 288, respectively. Static gain, normalized averaging, response shuffling, and same-bit binary-state feedback with only its coefficient tuned under the same nonmemory parameters do not reproduce the full saturated-response benefit. Trajectory analysis links the improvement to acquisition of the channel-consistent valid-codeword basin and enhanced post-acquisition stability; the acquisition advantage persists from random and channel-hard-decision starts under the tested conditions. Across all 30 independent code realizations, fixed additive parameter-and-readout packages achieve lower bit- and frame-error rates than separately optimized pSA-specific packages, with neither package retuned for individual codes. These results show that the computational effect of temporal state depends on the retained quantity and its reinjection into stochastic dynamics.

</details>


### [193] [Confusion-Erasure Bounds of Error-Bounded Decoders under QAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.12631)
*Wenwen Chen,Bin Han,Hans D. Schotten*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究有限码长（FBL）条件下方形QAM调制的混淆错误与擦除概率，推导块混淆率的上下界，并表明在高可靠性场景中可检测擦除通常主导残余错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向6G超可靠低时延通信（URLLC），传统块错误率（BLER）将不可检测的块混淆与可检测的块擦除混为一谈，无法反映二者对系统可靠性的不同影响。现有误差有界译码分析需要扩展到方形QAM及有限码长场景，而QAM中不同符号对具有不同欧氏距离，导致混淆概率分析更复杂。

Method: 将误差有界译码的混淆—擦除分析推广到方形QAM的有限块长 regime。针对QAM符号对欧氏距离呈分布而非固定值的问题，分别采用两种处理方式推导块混淆率的解析界：下界将距离分布等效为其均方根（RMS）距离，上界则对不同距离对应的成对混淆概率进行平均。进一步证明这些界关于平均符号能量和块长均单调递减，且下降速率受星座阶数影响。

Result: 理论结果表明，提高平均符号能量或增加块长都会降低块混淆率，且星座阶数决定其下降特性。数值结果显示，随着信噪比（SNR）或冗余增加，混淆率可降低至远低于目标可靠性水平，残余错误主要由可检测的擦除事件构成。

Conclusion: 在高可靠、有限码长的QAM通信系统中，仅使用BLER评估可靠性是不充分的，应区分块混淆与块擦除。研究表明，当系统资源足够时，不可检测混淆可以被压低多个数量级，而擦除成为主要残余风险，因此系统设计和可靠性指标应重点关注擦除概率及其代价。

Abstract: 6G is expected to push ultra-reliable low-latency communication (URLLC) toward stringent residual-error targets for mission-critical services, where undetected errors and erasures carry fundamentally different costs. Block error rate (BLER) conflates block confusions (undetected errors) and block erasures, which have fundamentally different impacts on system reliability. This paper extends the confusion and erasure analysis of error-bounded decoders to square quadrature amplitude modulation (QAM) constellations in the finite blocklength (FBL) regime. To handle QAM's heterogeneous symbol energies - which make the per-pair Euclidean distance a distribution rather than a single value - we derive analytical lower and upper bounds on the block confusion rate by, respectively, collapsing this distribution to its root-mean-square (RMS) distance and averaging the pairwise confusion over it. These bounds are proven to be monotonically decreasing in both the average symbol energy and the blocklength, with the decrease rate governed by the constellation order. Numerical results confirm that as the signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) or redundancy increases, the confusion rate falls many orders of magnitude below the reliability target, leaving detectable erasures as the dominant residual error.

</details>
